FILE: GridSense_Final_Report.md
================================================
# GridSense: Smart EV Charging and Load Management

Team 3 - Submission report

## Objective

Demonstrate a complete distributed IoT feedback loop for a simulated EV charging
station: sensor observations, MQTT transport, live visualization, persistent data,
evaluated machine learning, control decisions and actuator acknowledgements.

## Architecture and deployment

The supplied architecture image is `refererncesss.png`. One laptop runs the Python
sensor/plant simulator and attached actuator; the second runs Aedes on TCP 1883,
Node-RED, SQLite, the analytics/decision service and the dashboard. This describes
the retained local/LAN mode. The current public-broker mode instead connects both
laptops to `broker.emqx.io:8883` over verified TLS, using the shared prefix in
`mqtt-public.json`. No local MQTT broker is started in public mode.

1. `simulator.py` publishes JSON observations on `ev/telemetry` every two seconds.
2. Node-RED receives observations through MQTT with QoS 1.
3. Independent branches update the dashboard and POST observations to `/store`.
4. SQLite commits the observation and returns its run/sequence key. `/decide` reads
   the committed row, performs inference and builds a control plan.
5. Node-RED publishes that plan on `ev/actuator` through the same broker.
6. The sensor laptop validates and applies the plan and publishes `ev/ack`.
7. Node-RED stores acknowledgements for closed-loop evaluation. Subsequent readings
   reflect the action applied to the simulated plant.

The Python HTTP adapter is internal to the project laptop. MQTT connects the two
laptops; neither the dashboard nor database needs to be exposed to the LAN. The
live display continues independently when analytics is unavailable. The plant
rejects invalid, duplicate or out-of-order plans and expires stale commands.

## Simulation and scheduling

The station has six EVs, two charger sockets, a 10 kW rated supply and 1 kW base
load. A 5% reserve produces a 9.5 kW operating limit. Arrivals, departure deadlines,
remaining energy, battery state and charge-rate limits constrain scheduling.
The fallback scheduler uses feasibility-aware earliest departure priority, stable
socket assignments, dwell time and a power deadband. The tariff scheduler uses
constrained optimization to defer flexible demand while respecting deadlines;
solver failure falls back to deadline scheduling. Tariffs are illustrative INR/kWh.

The `--tou-demo` scenario provides feasible evening requests. The default congested
scenario deliberately includes demand that cannot all be served by its deadlines.

## Data sources

ACN-Data is the primary configured source: real Caltech workplace charging sessions,
including connection/departure times, delivered energy and charging-current traces.
Reference: Zachary J. Lee, Tongxin Li and Steven H. Low, "ACN-Data: Analysis and
Applications of an Open EV Charging Dataset," ACM e-Energy, 2019.
Dataset and registration: https://ev.caltech.edu/dataset.

Set ACN_DATA_TOKEN on the project laptop. The latest available complete window is
queried and cached for 24 hours; this is historical data, not today's live traffic.
Missing credentials, unavailable service or fewer than 18 observed days trigger the
clearly logged synthetic fallback. No token or raw session cache is shipped.

SoC is simulated from real current traces and delivered energy. Where vehicle
identity is available, usable battery capacity comes from the OpenEV Data public
JSON release (https://open-ev-data.github.io/latest/dataset/HOW_TO_CONSUME/).
ACN normally does not identify the vehicle, so an explicitly logged 40 kWh assumption
is often necessary. Initial SoC uses requested energy when available, otherwise an
assumption informed by taper. The current integral is normalized to delivered energy;
SoC is capped at 100%, and excess energy is reported. These are model-derived
trajectories, not measured battery SoC. The CC/CV taper above 80% is simplified.
Tariffs and the separate ToU scenario remain illustrative simulations.

## Machine learning and evaluation

History replay sends ACN hourly demand rows through MQTT and Node-RED to SQLite.
Each session's total energy is assigned to its arrival hour; this predicts arriving
energy demand, not measured instantaneous station load. Missing arrival bins inside
a fully retrieved interval are zeros. Training reads one dataset from SQLite, requires
18 complete days, and uses chronological training/validation/test splits. The retained
fallback generates 1,008 rows over 42 days (28/7/7 split). Each classification split
must contain both classes; unsuitable real windows produce an explicit training error.

The current recorded metrics below use **synthetic-fallback**. They are not real ACN
prediction scores. The report's data_source field and /health expose provenance.

Five model families cover the three categories in the diagram:

- Traditional: decision tree and support vector regression/classification.
- Ensemble: random forest and XGBoost.
- Neural network: feed-forward ANN with hidden layers 64, 32 and 16.

Each family includes an energy regressor and an independently trained classifier
for high demand (at least 30 kWh per hour). Regression metrics are RMSE, MAE and R2;
classification metrics include accuracy, precision, recall and F1. Computational
metrics include training time, warmed inference latency, serialized size and peak
Python-tracked allocations. Tracked memory is not total process/GPU memory.

Selection uses validation RMSE subject to a 100 ms p95 inference limit and improvement
against the constant baseline. Test scores do not select models. The current selected
model is **traditional_decision_tree**. Models are loaded and warmed at service startup.
Without a bundle, the application explicitly reports `untrained_safe_rules`.

| Model | Test RMSE (kWh) | MAE (kWh) | R2 | Accuracy | F1 |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| traditional_decision_tree | 13.155 | 10.399 | 0.532 | 0.917 | 0.788 |
| ensemble_random_forest | 12.824 | 10.193 | 0.555 | 0.893 | 0.757 |
| deep_ann | 13.007 | 10.214 | 0.543 | 0.851 | 0.706 |
| traditional_svr | 14.539 | 11.414 | 0.428 | 0.911 | 0.776 |
| ensemble_xgboost | 12.706 | 9.976 | 0.564 | 0.917 | 0.788 |

Exact metrics, splits and warnings are in `artifacts/ml_comparison.json`; held-out
predictions are in `artifacts/classification_predictions.json`.

## Verified results

The recorded local integration test (`artifacts/live_check.json`) verified:

- 1,008 active fallback observations persisted through MQTT/Node-RED; the database
  also contains a separate 1,008-row mocked ACN dataset from integration testing.
- 5 live cycles and 5 acknowledgements stored.
- 5 accepted commands and changed actuator telemetry.
- Dashboard HTTP status 200.
- Peak observed site load 9.5 kW.
- Mean sensor-to-actuator response 40.61 ms;
  p95 response 53.01 ms.

A separate fixture-based ACN integration test (`artifacts/live_check_acn_mock.json`)
passed MQTT ingestion, five-model training, five control cycles and five acknowledgements.
These ACN-shaped fixtures prove wiring and provenance handling, not live ACN access.
No ACN token was configured during verification, so authenticated fetching remains
unverified. Unit tests mock all ACN and capacity requests and require no network.

This is a short local-loop check, not a sustained or two-physical-laptop network
benchmark. Cross-laptop latency requires synchronized system clocks.

The offline fallback-policy comparison (`control_comparison.json`) meets four of
six congested targets, supplies 65.5% of requested energy and records zero capacity
violations or rapid switches. The feasible low-energy comparison
(`control_feasible.json`) meets all targets. These scenarios do not establish a
control-outcome advantage of ML over the constant baseline.

The separate evening tariff comparison (`tou_comparison.json`) delivers 16 kWh
and meets all deadlines. Deadline-only charging costs INR 192; tariff-aware charging
costs INR 84 (56.25% lower). Peak site demand is 9.5 kW and 8 kW respectively. This
result is specific to the synthetic scenario and illustrative prices. The unmanaged
counterfactual reaches 15 kW and intentionally has no site cap.

## Public-broker verification

A separate real-internet test is recorded in `artifacts/public_mqtt_check.json`.
It verified TLS, five accepted commands, six stored cycles and six stored
acknowledgements through `broker.emqx.io:8883`, with the trained decision-tree
model, dashboard HTTP 200 and a 9.5 kW observed peak. Mean observed response was
approximately 700 ms. Both test clients ran locally while using the real internet
broker; connectivity between the user's two physical laptops remains to be tested.

Public topics are not access-controlled. The unique namespace reduces collisions
but does not prevent others reading data or injecting messages. TLS protects the
connection, not topic privacy. The recorded broker test used synthetic data. Do not
transmit credentials or personal information on public topics; real history replay
contains hourly aggregates, and prepared fleets contain derived simulation parameters.

## Reproduction and limitations

Follow `README.md` for setup, the two-laptop commands, history replay, training and
verification. The test suite checks storage-before-inference, architecture wiring,
model selection, command rejection, safety limits, scheduling and tariff behavior.
`tests/live_check.py` runs the local broker/Node-RED/service/simulator loop.

The sensor and actuator are simulated, socket reassignment is instantaneous and
battery taper is simplified. Real charging behavior is represented when ACN is
configured, but SoC remains model-derived. The ANN is a multilayer feed-forward network, not an
LSTM. Real-world prediction accuracy and physical-hardware control are unverified.
The demo broker has no authentication or TLS and is intended for a trusted LAN.
The submission installer trains models using the destination laptop dependencies,
avoiding cross-version pickle incompatibilities. Use Python 3.10 or later; Python 3.11
was checked. Dependency downloads require internet even for a synthetic demo. Actual grading/upload requirements remain
those of the receiving institution.

FILE: ml_train.py
================================================
"""Five-model comparison. ALL training rows are SELECTed from SQLite.

Target: requested energy in the forecast hour (kWh).
Classification: independently trained high-demand alarm, energy >= 30 kWh.
No random split: first 2/3 of days train, next 1/6 validate, last 1/6 test.
"""
import argparse
import json
import pickle
import time
import tracemalloc
import warnings
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
from sklearn.tree import DecisionTreeRegressor, DecisionTreeClassifier
from sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestRegressor, RandomForestClassifier
from sklearn.neural_network import MLPRegressor, MLPClassifier
from sklearn.svm import SVR, SVC
from sklearn.pipeline import make_pipeline
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
from sklearn.compose import TransformedTargetRegressor
from sklearn.metrics import (accuracy_score, precision_score, recall_score, f1_score,
                             mean_absolute_error, mean_squared_error, r2_score,
                             balanced_accuracy_score, confusion_matrix, average_precision_score)
from station_store import DB, database

ARTIFACTS = Path(__file__).with_name('artifacts')
THRESHOLD_KWH = 30.0


def features(day, hour):
    # Calendar features are known BEFORE the forecast hour starts.
    return [hour, np.sin(2*np.pi*hour/24), np.cos(2*np.pi*hour/24), day % 7]


def scores(y, pred, probabilities=None):
    actual_class = y >= THRESHOLD_KWH
    predicted_class = pred >= THRESHOLD_KWH if probabilities is None else probabilities >= .5
    return {'accuracy': float(accuracy_score(actual_class, predicted_class)),
            'precision': float(precision_score(actual_class, predicted_class, zero_division=0)),
            'recall': float(recall_score(actual_class, predicted_class, zero_division=0)),
            'f1': float(f1_score(actual_class, predicted_class, zero_division=0)),
            'balanced_accuracy': float(balanced_accuracy_score(actual_class,predicted_class)),
            'confusion_matrix_tn_fp_fn_tp': confusion_matrix(actual_class,predicted_class,labels=[False,True]).ravel().tolist(),
            'average_precision': float(average_precision_score(actual_class,probabilities if probabilities is not None else pred)),
            'rmse_kwh': float(np.sqrt(mean_squared_error(y, pred))),
            'mae_kwh': float(mean_absolute_error(y, pred)), 'r2': float(r2_score(y, pred)),
            'positive_samples': int(actual_class.sum()), 'negative_samples':int((~actual_class).sum()),
            'majority_baseline_accuracy':float(max(actual_class.mean(),1-actual_class.mean())),
            'samples': len(y)}


def train(db=DB, output=ARTIFACTS, dataset=None):
    with database(db) as conn:
        if dataset is None:
            latest = conn.execute('SELECT dataset FROM demand_samples ORDER BY rowid DESC LIMIT 1').fetchone()
            dataset = latest['dataset'] if latest else 'acn-caltech-v1'
        rows = conn.execute('SELECT day,hour,energy_kwh,source FROM demand_samples WHERE dataset=? ORDER BY day,hour', (dataset,)).fetchall()
    days = sorted({r['day'] for r in rows})
    if len(days) < 18 or any(sum(r['day'] == d for r in rows) != 24 for d in days):
        raise ValueError(f'Need at least 18 complete days for {dataset} in SQLite. Run simulator.py --history through Node-RED first.')
    first, second = len(days)*2//3, len(days)*5//6
    groups = [set(days[:first]), set(days[first:second]), set(days[second:])]
    splits = []
    for group in groups:
        subset = [r for r in rows if r['day'] in group]
        splits.append((np.array([features(r['day'],r['hour']) for r in subset]),
                       np.array([r['energy_kwh'] for r in subset])))
    (x,y),(xv,yv),(xt,yt) = splits
    for label, values in [('train',y),('validation',yv),('test',yt)]:
        if len(np.unique(values >= THRESHOLD_KWH)) != 2:
            raise ValueError(f'{label} must contain both demand classes; collect more representative data')
    from xgboost import XGBRegressor, XGBClassifier
    boost_options = dict(n_estimators=120, max_depth=3, learning_rate=.05,
                         subsample=.9, colsample_bytree=1., tree_method='hist',
                         n_jobs=1, random_state=42)
    models = {
        'traditional_decision_tree': DecisionTreeRegressor(max_depth=6, min_samples_leaf=8, random_state=42),
        'ensemble_random_forest': RandomForestRegressor(n_estimators=80, max_depth=8, min_samples_leaf=4, n_jobs=1, random_state=42),
        'deep_ann': TransformedTargetRegressor(
            regressor=make_pipeline(StandardScaler(), MLPRegressor(hidden_layer_sizes=(64,32,16),
                activation='relu', solver='adam', max_iter=600, random_state=42, early_stopping=False)),
            transformer=StandardScaler()),
        'traditional_svr': TransformedTargetRegressor(
            regressor=make_pipeline(StandardScaler(), SVR(C=10., epsilon=.1, kernel='rbf')),
            transformer=StandardScaler()),
        'ensemble_xgboost': XGBRegressor(objective='reg:squarederror', **boost_options)}
    classifiers = {
        'traditional_decision_tree':DecisionTreeClassifier(max_depth=6,min_samples_leaf=8,class_weight='balanced',random_state=42),
        'ensemble_random_forest':RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=80,max_depth=8,min_samples_leaf=4,class_weight='balanced_subsample',n_jobs=1,random_state=42),
        'deep_ann':make_pipeline(StandardScaler(),MLPClassifier(hidden_layer_sizes=(64,32,16),
                     max_iter=600,random_state=42,early_stopping=False)),
        'traditional_svr': make_pipeline(StandardScaler(), SVC(
            C=10., kernel='rbf', probability=True, class_weight='balanced', random_state=42)),
        'ensemble_xgboost': XGBClassifier(objective='binary:logistic', eval_metric='logloss',
            scale_pos_weight=float(np.sum(y < THRESHOLD_KWH)/np.sum(y >= THRESHOLD_KWH)),
            **boost_options)}
    yc=(y>=THRESHOLD_KWH).astype(int)
    # Oversample ONLY the ANN training split. Validation/test prevalence stays untouched.
    rng=np.random.default_rng(42)
    groups_by_class=[np.flatnonzero(yc==c) for c in (0,1)]
    count=max(map(len,groups_by_class))
    balanced_indices=np.concatenate([np.concatenate([g,rng.choice(g,count-len(g),replace=True)]) for g in groups_by_class])
    rng.shuffle(balanced_indices)
    output = Path(output)
    output.mkdir(exist_ok=True, parents=True)
    comparisons = {}
    test_probabilities = {}
    for name, model in models.items():
        classifier=classifiers[name]
        tracemalloc.start()
        start = time.perf_counter()
        with warnings.catch_warnings(record=True) as caught:
            warnings.simplefilter('always')
            model.fit(x,y)
            if name=='deep_ann':
                classifier.fit(x[balanced_indices],yc[balanced_indices])
            else:
                classifier.fit(x,yc)
        training_time = time.perf_counter()-start
        _, peak = tracemalloc.get_traced_memory()
        tracemalloc.stop()
        # Warm-up excluded; serial single-sample end-to-end model prediction latency.
        model.predict(xv[:1])
        timings = []
        for i in range(50):
            start = time.perf_counter()
            model.predict(xv[i % len(xv):i % len(xv)+1])
            timings.append((time.perf_counter()-start)*1000)
        blob = pickle.dumps(model, protocol=pickle.HIGHEST_PROTOCOL)
        classifier_blob=pickle.dumps(classifier,protocol=pickle.HIGHEST_PROTOCOL)
        (output/f'{name}.pkl').write_bytes(blob)
        (output/f'{name}_classifier.pkl').write_bytes(classifier_blob)
        pv=classifier.predict_proba(xv)[:,1]
        pt=classifier.predict_proba(xt)[:,1]
        test_probabilities[name]=pt
        comparisons[name] = {'validation': scores(yv, np.maximum(0,model.predict(xv)),pv),
            'test': scores(yt,np.maximum(0,model.predict(xt)),pt),
            'thresholded_regression_test': scores(yt,np.maximum(0,model.predict(xt))),
            'computational': {'training_seconds': training_time,
                'inference_mean_ms': float(np.mean(timings)), 'inference_p95_ms': float(np.percentile(timings,95)),
                'peak_traced_memory_bytes': peak, 'model_size_bytes': len(blob)+len(classifier_blob),
                'regressor_size_bytes':len(blob),'classifier_size_bytes':len(classifier_blob),
                'measurement_scope':'Training/memory/size include regressor + classifier. Control inference measures regressor only.'},
            'warnings': [str(w.message) for w in caught]}
    baseline = float(np.mean(y))
    baseline_val = scores(yv, np.full_like(yv,baseline))
    eligible = [n for n,m in comparisons.items() if m['computational']['inference_p95_ms'] <= 100
                and m['validation']['rmse_kwh'] <= baseline_val['rmse_kwh']]
    selected = min(eligible, key=lambda n:comparisons[n]['validation']['rmse_kwh']) if eligible else 'constant_baseline'
    report = {'created_at':time.time(), 'dataset':dataset, 'source': sorted({r['source'] for r in rows}),
              'data_source': 'acn-data-caltech' if rows[0]['source'] == 'acn-data-caltech' else 'synthetic-fallback',
              'training_days':days[:first], 'validation_days':days[first:second], 'test_days':days[second:],
              'classification_definition': f'Actual energy >= {THRESHOLD_KWH} kWh; independent classifier probability >=0.5. Threshold fixed before test evaluation.',
              'imbalance_handling':'Balanced tree/forest/SVC weights; XGBoost training-only class ratio; ANN training-only oversampling; validation/test unchanged.',
              'test_binary_disagreements':{a:{b:int(np.count_nonzero((pa>=.5)!=(pb>=.5))) for b,pb in test_probabilities.items()} for a,pa in test_probabilities.items()},
              'memory_definition':'Peak tracemalloc-tracked allocations during fit, not total process RSS or GPU memory',
              'selection_rule':'Lowest validation RMSE among models beating constant baseline and p95 inference <=100ms; test set never selects model',
              'models':comparisons, 'baseline_validation':baseline_val,
              'baseline_test':scores(yt,np.full_like(yt,baseline)), 'selected_model':selected}
    bundle = {'model':models.get(selected), 'baseline':baseline, 'report':report}
    # Atomic publication prevents a running service reading a partial pickle.
    tmp = output/'bundle.tmp'
    tmp.write_bytes(pickle.dumps(bundle))
    tmp.replace(output/'bundle.pkl')
    (output/'ml_comparison.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    (output/'classification_predictions.json').write_text(json.dumps({'actual_high_demand':(yt>=THRESHOLD_KWH).astype(int).tolist(),
        'probabilities':{n:p.tolist() for n,p in test_probabilities.items()}},indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    with database(db) as conn:
        conn.execute('INSERT INTO ml_evaluations VALUES (?,?)',(report['created_at'],json.dumps(report)))
    return report


def predict(bundle, hour):
    day, h = divmod(int(hour)+1,24)
    start = time.perf_counter()
    value = bundle['baseline'] if bundle['model'] is None else float(bundle['model'].predict([features(day,h)])[0])
    return max(0,value), (time.perf_counter()-start)*1000


if __name__ == '__main__':
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument('--db',type=Path,default=DB)
    parser.add_argument('--output',type=Path,default=ARTIFACTS)
    parser.add_argument('--dataset', help='Dataset in SQLite (default: most recently ingested; e.g. acn-caltech-v1)')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    report = train(args.db,args.output,args.dataset)
    print(json.dumps(report,indent=2))

FILE: package_submission.py
================================================
"""Build the demo-only ZIP from an explicit runtime allowlist."""
import hashlib
import json
import shutil
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path
from zipfile import ZipFile, ZIP_DEFLATED

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
FILES = [
    'setup_demo.ps1', 'start_demo.ps1',
    'requirements-station.txt', 'prepare_demo.py',
    'acn_data.py', 'simulator.py',
    'mqtt-public.json',
    'sender/simulator.py', 'sender/actuator.py', 'sender/station_core.py',
    'sender/tariff.py', 'sender/mqtt-public.json',
    'station_store.py', 'station_service.py', 'decision_engine.py',
    'ml_train.py', 'tou_scheduler.py',
    'node-red/flows.json', 'node-red/settings.js', 'node-red/build-public-flow.cjs',
]


def build():
    paths = {name: ROOT/name for name in FILES}
    paths['README.md'] = ROOT/'DEMO_README.md'
    missing = [str(p) for p in paths.values() if not p.is_file()]
    if missing:
        raise FileNotFoundError('Required runtime files missing: ' + ', '.join(missing))
    if json.loads((ROOT/'mqtt-public.json').read_text()) != json.loads((ROOT/'sender/mqtt-public.json').read_text()):
        raise ValueError('Project and sender MQTT profiles must match')
    # Keep presentation tooling and local-broker dev dependencies out of the demo.
    staging = ROOT/'submission'/'runtime-node'
    staging.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    package = json.loads((ROOT/'node-red/package.json').read_text())
    package.pop('devDependencies', None)
    package['scripts'] = {'start': 'node-red --userDir . --settings settings.js flows.public.json'}
    (staging/'package.json').write_text(json.dumps(package, indent=2))
    (staging/'package-lock.json').write_bytes((ROOT/'node-red/package-lock.json').read_bytes())
    npm = shutil.which('npm.cmd') or shutil.which('npm')
    if not npm:
        raise RuntimeError('npm is required to prepare the runtime dependency lock')
    subprocess.run([npm, 'install', '--package-lock-only', '--ignore-scripts', '--offline',
                    '--no-audit', '--no-fund', '--prefix', str(staging)], check=True)
    for name in ('package.json', 'package-lock.json'):
        paths['node-red/'+name] = staging/name
    content = {name: path.read_bytes() for name, path in paths.items()}
    # Explicit allowlist excludes .env, ACN caches, downloaded sessions and tokens.
    if any(Path(name).name.startswith('.env') for name in content):
        raise ValueError('Environment files are forbidden in the submission')
    output = ROOT/'submission'/'GridSense_Team3.zip'
    temporary = output.with_suffix('.zip.tmp')
    with ZipFile(temporary, 'w', ZIP_DEFLATED) as archive:
        for name, data in sorted(content.items()):
            archive.writestr('GridSense/'+name, data)
    with ZipFile(temporary) as archive:
        if archive.testzip() is not None:
            raise RuntimeError('ZIP integrity check failed')
        for name, data in content.items():
            if hashlib.sha256(archive.read('GridSense/'+name)).digest() != hashlib.sha256(data).digest():
                raise RuntimeError('Checksum mismatch: '+name)
    temporary.replace(output)
    print(output)
    print(f'{len(content)} runtime/demo files; {output.stat().st_size:,} bytes; integrity verified.')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    build()



================================================
FILE: prepare_demo.py
================================================
"""Train compatible demo models locally using synthetic history staged in SQLite.

This is offline installation-time preparation, not live sensor ingestion. The
running application still uses MQTT -> Node-RED -> SQLite -> decision -> MQTT.
"""
import json
import shutil
import sys
import tempfile
from importlib.metadata import version
from pathlib import Path

from sender.simulator import history_payload
from station_store import ingest
from ml_train import train


def prepare(root=None, synthetic_only=False):
    root = Path(root) if root is not None else Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    payload = history_payload(synthetic_only=synthetic_only)
    print('Preparing five models for this Python environment; please wait...', flush=True)
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='gridsense-setup-') as tmp:
        temporary = Path(tmp)
        db = temporary/'training.db'
        ingest(payload, db)
        report = train(db, temporary/'models')
        target = root/'artifacts'
        target.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        pending = target/'bundle.tmp'
        shutil.copyfile(temporary/'models/bundle.pkl', pending)
        pending.replace(target/'bundle.pkl')
        shutil.copyfile(temporary/'models/ml_comparison.json', target/'ml_comparison.json')
    metadata = {'python': sys.version.split()[0],
                'packages': {p: version(p) for p in ('numpy', 'scikit-learn', 'scipy', 'xgboost')},
                'selected_model': report['selected_model'], 'training_rows': len(payload['samples']),
                'data_source':report['data_source'],
                'preparation': 'installation-time history staged in temporary SQLite (live ingestion uses MQTT)'}
    (target/'runtime.json').write_text(json.dumps(metadata, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
    print('Local models ready. Selected: '+report['selected_model'], flush=True)
    return metadata


if __name__ == '__main__':
    import argparse
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument('--synthetic-only', action='store_true')
    prepare(synthetic_only=parser.parse_args().synthetic_only)

param([switch]$SyntheticOnly)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
Set-Location $PSScriptRoot
Get-Command python -ErrorAction Stop | Out-Null
Get-Command node -ErrorAction Stop | Out-Null
Get-Command npm.cmd -ErrorAction Stop | Out-Null
if (-not (Test-Path '.venv/Scripts/python.exe')) {
    & python -m venv .venv
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Could not create the Python environment.' }
}
& '.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe' -c 'import sys; sys.exit(0 if sys.version_info >= (3,10) else "Python 3.10 or newer is required")'
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'The existing .venv needs Python 3.10 or newer.' }
& '.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe' -m pip install -r requirements-station.txt
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Python dependency installation failed. See the Python compatibility instructions in README.md.' }
& npm.cmd ci --prefix node-red --omit=dev --no-audit --no-fund
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Node-RED dependency installation failed.' }
if ($SyntheticOnly) {
    & '.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe' prepare_demo.py --synthetic-only
} else {
    & '.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe' prepare_demo.py
}
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Local model preparation failed. Check the error above.' }
Write-Host 'Setup complete. Run ./start_demo.ps1.'

FILE: start.sh

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")"
python station_service.py > service.log 2>&1 & service_pid=$!
(cd node-red && exec node node_modules/node-red/red.js --userDir . --settings settings.js flows.json) > nodered.log 2>&1 & red_pid=$!
python simulator.py > station.log 2>&1 & simulator_pid=$!
trap 'kill "$service_pid" "$red_pid" "$simulator_pid" 2>/dev/null || true' EXIT INT TERM
echo "Dashboard: http://127.0.0.1:1880/dashboard/station"
wait -n



================================================
FILE: start_demo.ps1
================================================
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
Set-Location $PSScriptRoot
$pythonPath = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot '.venv/Scripts/python.exe'
if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $pythonPath) -or -not (Test-Path 'node-red/node_modules/node-red/red.js')) {
    throw 'Run ./setup_demo.ps1 first.'
}
$nodePath = (Get-Command node).Source
& $nodePath 'node-red/build-public-flow.cjs'
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Public MQTT flow generation failed.' }
$workers = @()
try {
    $workers += Start-Process $pythonPath -ArgumentList 'station_service.py' -WorkingDirectory $PSScriptRoot -WindowStyle Hidden -PassThru -RedirectStandardOutput 'service.log' -RedirectStandardError 'service.err.log'
    $workers += Start-Process $nodePath -ArgumentList 'node_modules/node-red/red.js --userDir . --settings settings.js flows.public.json' -WorkingDirectory "$PSScriptRoot/node-red" -WindowStyle Hidden -PassThru -RedirectStandardOutput "$PSScriptRoot/nodered.log" -RedirectStandardError "$PSScriptRoot/nodered.err.log"
    Write-Host 'Dashboard: http://127.0.0.1:1880/dashboard/station'
    Write-Host 'On the sensor laptop, inside sender/: python simulator.py --public-broker'
    Write-Host 'Keep this window open. Press Ctrl+C to stop.'
    while ($true) {
        Start-Sleep -Seconds 2
        foreach ($worker in $workers) {
            $worker.Refresh()
            if ($worker.HasExited) { throw 'A service exited. Check service.err.log and nodered.err.log.' }
        }
    }
} finally {
    foreach ($worker in $workers) {
        if (-not $worker.HasExited) { Stop-Process -Id $worker.Id -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue }
    }
}



================================================
FILE: start_station.ps1
================================================
param([switch]$TouDemo, [switch]$RemoteSensor, [switch]$CloudBroker, [switch]$PublicBroker)
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
Set-Location $PSScriptRoot
if (-not (Test-Path "node-red/node_modules/node-red/red.js")) {
    throw "Run npm.cmd install --prefix node-red first."
}
$pythonPath = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot ".venv/Scripts/python.exe"
if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $pythonPath)) { $pythonPath = (Get-Command python).Source }
$simulatorArgs = if ($TouDemo) { "simulator.py --tou-demo" } else { "simulator.py" }
$nodePath = (Get-Command node).Source
$workers = @()
$flowName = 'flows.json'
if ($PublicBroker) {
    & $nodePath 'node-red/build-public-flow.cjs'
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Failed to generate public MQTT flow.' }
    $flowName = 'flows.public.json'
    $CloudBroker = $true
}
if ($CloudBroker) { $RemoteSensor = $true }
try {
    if (-not $CloudBroker) {
    $probe = [System.Net.Sockets.TcpClient]::new()
    try { $probe.Connect('127.0.0.1', 1883); $brokerReady = $true }
    catch { $brokerReady = $false }
    finally { $probe.Dispose() }
    if (-not $brokerReady) {
        $brokerArgs = if ($RemoteSensor) { "node-red/demo-broker.cjs --lan" } else { "node-red/demo-broker.cjs" }
        $workers += Start-Process $nodePath -ArgumentList $brokerArgs -WorkingDirectory $PSScriptRoot -WindowStyle Hidden -PassThru -RedirectStandardOutput "broker.log" -RedirectStandardError "broker.err.log"
    } elseif ($RemoteSensor) {
        Write-Warning "An existing broker is using port 1883. Ensure it accepts LAN connections; this launcher cannot change its listener."
    }
    }
    $workers += Start-Process $pythonPath -ArgumentList "station_service.py" -WorkingDirectory $PSScriptRoot -WindowStyle Hidden -PassThru -RedirectStandardOutput "service.log" -RedirectStandardError "service.err.log"
    $workers += Start-Process $nodePath -ArgumentList "node_modules/node-red/red.js --userDir . --settings settings.js $flowName" -WorkingDirectory "$PSScriptRoot/node-red" -WindowStyle Hidden -PassThru -RedirectStandardOutput "$PSScriptRoot/nodered.log" -RedirectStandardError "$PSScriptRoot/nodered.err.log"
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 3
    if (-not $RemoteSensor) {
        $workers += Start-Process $pythonPath -ArgumentList $simulatorArgs -WorkingDirectory $PSScriptRoot -WindowStyle Hidden -PassThru -RedirectStandardOutput "station.log" -RedirectStandardError "station.err.log"
    } elseif ($PublicBroker) {
        Write-Host "On the sensor laptop: python simulator.py --public-broker (copy mqtt-public.json with the sensor files)."
    } elseif ($CloudBroker) {
        Write-Host "Cloud MQTT mode: configure the broker address, TLS and credentials in Node-RED. No local broker or sensor is started."
    } else {
        Write-Host "Waiting for the remote sensor. On the sensor laptop: python simulator.py --broker-host <this-laptop-LAN-IP>"
    }
    Write-Host "Dashboard: http://127.0.0.1:1880/dashboard/station"
    Write-Host "Node-RED editor: http://127.0.0.1:1880"
    Write-Host "Press Ctrl+C to stop this run."
    while ($true) {
        Start-Sleep -Seconds 2
        foreach ($worker in $workers) {
            $worker.Refresh()
            if ($worker.HasExited) { throw "A service exited. See *.err.log files." }
        }
    }
} finally {
    foreach ($worker in $workers) {
        if (-not $worker.HasExited) { Stop-Process -Id $worker.Id -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue }
    }
}



================================================
FILE: station_service.py
================================================
"""Node-RED HTTP persistence/evaluation boundary. Never publishes MQTT itself."""
import json
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer
from station_store import DB, database, ingest
from decision_engine import decide, initialize_models


def closed_loop(run_id,path=DB):
    with database(path) as conn:
        states = [json.loads(r['telemetry']) for r in conn.execute('SELECT telemetry FROM cycles WHERE run_id=? ORDER BY sequence',(run_id,))]
        acks = [json.loads(r['payload']) for r in conn.execute('SELECT payload FROM actuator_acks WHERE run_id=?',(run_id,))]
    if not states:
        return {}
    last = states[-1]
    departed = [e for e in last['vehicles'] if e['status']=='departed']
    required = sum(e['delivered_kwh']+e['remaining_kwh'] for e in departed)
    latencies = sorted(a['response_ms'] for a in acks if a['accepted'])
    inference = [a['inference_ms'] for a in acks if a['accepted'] and a.get('inference_ms') is not None]
    decisions = [a['decision_ms'] for a in acks if a['accepted'] and a.get('decision_ms') is not None]
    return {'departed_vehicles':len(departed),
            'deadline_success_fraction':sum(e['remaining_kwh'] <= .01 for e in departed)/len(departed) if departed else None,
            'departed_energy_fulfillment_fraction':sum(e['delivered_kwh'] for e in departed)/required if required else None,
            'unmet_departure_kwh':sum(e['remaining_kwh'] for e in departed),
            'peak_site_kw':max(s['load_kw'] for s in states),
            'capacity_violation_samples':sum(s['load_kw'] > s['capacity_kw']+1e-6 for s in states),
            'operating_limit_kw':last['capacity_kw']*.95,
            'safety_margin_violation_samples':sum(s['load_kw'] > s['capacity_kw']*.95+1e-6 for s in states),
            'accepted_commands':len(latencies),'acknowledgements':len(acks),
            'response_mean_ms':sum(latencies)/len(latencies) if latencies else None,
            'response_p95_ms':latencies[min(len(latencies)-1,int(.95*len(latencies)))] if latencies else None,
            'mean_inference_ms':sum(inference)/len(inference) if inference else None,
            'mean_decision_ms':sum(decisions)/len(decisions) if decisions else None,
            'socket_switches':max((a.get('switch_count',0) for a in acks),default=0),
            'rapid_switches':max((a.get('rapid_switch_count',0) for a in acks),default=0)}


def process(payload,path=DB):
    key = ingest(payload,path)  # Separate committed transaction BEFORE decision/model input reads.
    if 'stored_history_rows' in key:
        return key
    result = decide(key,path)
    result['closed_loop'] = closed_loop(key['run_id'],path)
    return result


class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    def respond(self,data):
        body = json.dumps(data,allow_nan=False).encode()
        self.send_response(200)
        self.send_header('Content-Type','application/json')
        self.send_header('Content-Length',str(len(body)))
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(body)

    def do_GET(self):
        if self.path != '/health':
            self.send_error(404)
            return
        with database() as conn:
            count = conn.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM demand_samples').fetchone()[0]
            latest = conn.execute('SELECT dataset,source FROM demand_samples ORDER BY rowid DESC LIMIT 1').fetchone()
            active_count = conn.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM demand_samples WHERE dataset=?', (latest['dataset'],)).fetchone()[0] if latest else 0
        self.respond({'status':'ok','history_rows':count, 'active_history_rows':active_count,
                      'dataset':latest['dataset'] if latest else None,
                      'data_source':latest['source'] if latest else None})

    def do_POST(self):
        try:
            size = int(self.headers.get('Content-Length',0))
            if not 0 < size <= 1000000:
                raise ValueError('Invalid request size')
            payload = json.loads(self.rfile.read(size))
            if self.path == '/store':
                self.respond(ingest(payload))
            elif self.path == '/decide':
                result = decide(payload)
                result['closed_loop'] = closed_loop(payload['run_id'])
                self.respond(result)
            elif self.path == '/cycle':
                self.respond(process(payload))
            elif self.path == '/ack':
                with database() as conn:
                    # Keep successful application evidence if QoS redelivery later gets rejected.
                    conn.execute('INSERT INTO actuator_acks VALUES (?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(run_id,sequence) DO UPDATE SET payload=excluded.payload WHERE json_extract(actuator_acks.payload,\'$.accepted\')=0',
                                 (payload['run_id'],payload['sequence'],json.dumps(payload)))
                self.respond({'stored':True})
            else:
                self.send_error(404)
        except (ValueError,KeyError,TypeError,ZeroDivisionError) as exc:
            self.send_error(400,str(exc))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    initialize_models()
    print('GridSense SQLite/decision service: http://127.0.0.1:8765',flush=True)
    HTTPServer(('127.0.0.1',8765),Handler).serve_forever()

FILE: station_store.py
================================================
"""SQLite boundary: ingest commits before any analytics reads."""
import json
import sqlite3
from contextlib import contextmanager
from pathlib import Path

DB = Path(__file__).with_name('station.db')


@contextmanager
def database(path=DB):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(path, timeout=15)
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    try:
        conn.execute('PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL')
        conn.execute('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS demand_samples (dataset TEXT, day INTEGER, hour INTEGER, energy_kwh REAL, source TEXT, PRIMARY KEY(dataset,day,hour))')
        conn.execute('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS cycles (run_id TEXT, sequence INTEGER, hour REAL, telemetry TEXT, decision TEXT, PRIMARY KEY(run_id,sequence))')
        conn.execute('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS actuator_acks (run_id TEXT, sequence INTEGER, payload TEXT, PRIMARY KEY(run_id,sequence))')
        conn.execute('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ml_evaluations (created_at REAL PRIMARY KEY, report TEXT)')
        yield conn
        conn.commit()
    except Exception:
        conn.rollback()
        raise
    finally:
        conn.close()


def ingest(payload, path=DB):
    with database(path) as conn:
        if payload.get('kind') == 'history':
            rows = payload['samples']
            if len(rows) > 5000:
                raise ValueError('Too many history samples')
            import math
            for r in rows:
                if not (0 <= r['hour'] < 24 and r['day'] >= 0 and math.isfinite(r['energy_kwh']) and r['energy_kwh'] >= 0):
                    raise ValueError('Invalid history sample')
            # Each history message is a complete dataset snapshot. Replace it
            # atomically so a shorter real window cannot leave stale old days.
            conn.execute('DELETE FROM demand_samples WHERE dataset=?', (payload['dataset'],))
            # Refresh rowid order so /health describes the latest replay.
            conn.executemany('INSERT OR REPLACE INTO demand_samples VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)',
                             [(payload['dataset'], r['day'], r['hour'], r['energy_kwh'], payload['source']) for r in rows])
            if payload['source'] == 'synthetic-fallback':
                print('[FALLBACK] Using synthetic history (received and stored)', flush=True)
            return {'stored_history_rows': len(rows)}
        conn.execute('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO cycles VALUES (?,?,?,?,NULL)',
                     (payload['run_id'], payload['sequence'], payload['hour'], json.dumps(payload, allow_nan=False)))
    return {'run_id': payload['run_id'], 'sequence': payload['sequence']}


def read_state(key, path=DB):
    with database(path) as conn:
        row = conn.execute('SELECT telemetry FROM cycles WHERE run_id=? AND sequence=?',
                           (key['run_id'], key['sequence'])).fetchone()
        if not row:
            raise ValueError('Telemetry not committed to SQLite')
        return json.loads(row['telemetry'])



================================================
FILE: tou_scheduler.py
================================================
"""Receding-horizon cost minimization with hard energy/deadline/socket constraints.

Only the first control interval is applied. Replan from committed telemetry.
Infeasible requests or solver failures use the existing best-effort EDF plan.
"""
import math
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import Bounds, LinearConstraint, milp
from scipy.sparse import lil_matrix
from sender.station_core import site_limit_kw
from sender.tariff import price_at

CONTROL_HOURS = 1 / 30  # Must match simulator.py's two simulated minutes.


def apply_tou(state, plan, previous=None, enabled=True, time_limit=.5):
    now = state['hour']
    plan.update(price_per_kwh=price_at(now), price_currency='INR',
                tariff_profile=[price_at(h) for h in range(24)])
    if not enabled:
        plan['tou_status'] = 'disabled'
        return plan
    vehicles = [e for e in state['vehicles'] if e['departure'] > now
                and e['remaining_kwh'] > 1e-8]
    if not vehicles:
        plan.update(tou_status='idle', assignments=[])
        return plan
    available = max(0., site_limit_kw(state) - state['base_kw'])

    def fallback(reason):
        plan['tou_status'] = 'fallback_edf'
        plan['alerts'].append('ToU: ' + reason + '; using best-effort deadline scheduling')
        return plan

    # Conservative lower bound on taper efficiency until each vehicle's target.
    efficiency = [min(1., max(0., (1. - e['target']) / .2)) for e in vehicles]
    for e, eta in zip(vehicles, efficiency):
        if e['remaining_kwh'] > max(0., e['departure']-max(now, e['arrival'])) * min(e['max_kw'], available) * eta + 1e-6:
            return fallback('deadline feasibility cannot be certified (energy/rate/taper)')
    end = max(e['departure'] for e in vehicles)
    if end - now > 48 or len(vehicles) > 30:
        return fallback('planning horizon exceeds demo limits')
    # First interval matches the actuator cadence; later intervals <=15 minutes.
    first_end = min(now + CONTROL_HOURS, end)
    boundaries = {now, first_end, end}
    boundaries.update(t / 4 for t in range(math.ceil(first_end * 4), math.ceil(end * 4))
                      if first_end < t / 4 < end)
    for e in vehicles:
        boundaries.update(t for t in (e['arrival'], e['departure']) if first_end < t < end)
    edges = sorted(boundaries)
    durations = np.diff(edges)
    n, slots = len(vehicles), len(durations)
    count = n * slots
    # p[i,t] commanded kW and binary z[i,t] socket occupancy.
    upper = np.zeros(2 * count)
    objective = np.zeros(2 * count)
    for i, e in enumerate(vehicles):
        for t, duration in enumerate(durations):
            k = i * slots + t
            if e['arrival'] <= edges[t] + 1e-9 and edges[t+1] <= e['departure'] + 1e-9:
                upper[k], upper[count+k] = e['max_kw'], 1
            # Earlier charging wins equal-price ties. Physical load may taper below command.
            objective[k] = duration * (price_at(edges[t] + 1e-8) + 1e-4 * (edges[t]-now))
            objective[count+k] = 1e-7
    matrix = lil_matrix((n + 2 * slots + count, 2 * count))
    lower = np.full(matrix.shape[0], -np.inf)
    limit = np.zeros(matrix.shape[0])
    for i, e in enumerate(vehicles):
        matrix[i, i*slots:(i+1)*slots] = durations * efficiency[i]
        lower[i] = e['remaining_kwh']
        limit[i] = np.inf
    for t in range(slots):
        for i in range(n):
            k = i * slots + t
            matrix[n+t, k] = 1
            matrix[n+slots+t, count+k] = 1
        limit[n+t] = available
        limit[n+slots+t] = len(state['chargers'])
    for k in range(count):
        row = n + 2*slots + k
        matrix[row, k] = 1
        matrix[row, count+k] = -vehicles[k // slots]['max_kw']
    try:
        result = milp(objective, integrality=np.r_[np.zeros(count), np.ones(count)],
                      bounds=Bounds(np.zeros(2*count), upper),
                      constraints=LinearConstraint(matrix.tocsc(), lower, limit),
                      options={'time_limit': time_limit, 'mip_rel_gap': .001})
    except (ValueError, RuntimeError):
        return fallback('optimizer error')
    if result.x is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(result.x)):
        return fallback('no feasible plan returned within solver budget')
    # Validate even time-limited incumbents before relying on deadline feasibility.
    values = matrix @ result.x
    if (np.any(values < lower - 1e-6) or np.any(values > limit + 1e-6)
            or np.any(result.x < -1e-6) or np.any(result.x > upper + 1e-6)
            or np.any(abs(result.x[count:] - np.round(result.x[count:])) > 1e-6)):
        return fallback('solver result failed constraint validation')
    chosen = [(e, max(0., float(result.x[i*slots]))) for i, e in enumerate(vehicles)
              if result.x[i*slots] > 1e-7]
    if len(chosen) > len(state['chargers']):
        return fallback('invalid first-interval socket allocation')
    old = {a['ev_id']: a['charger_id'] for a in (previous or {}).get('assignments', [])}
    sockets = {e['id']: old[e['id']] for e, _ in chosen if old.get(e['id']) in state['chargers']}
    free = [c for c in state['chargers'] if c not in sockets.values()]
    assignments = []
    remaining = available
    for e, rate in chosen:
        # Only remove solver floating-point overshoot; do not round to 0.01 kW.
        rate = min(rate, remaining, e['max_kw'])
        remaining -= rate
        socket = sockets.get(e['id']) or free.pop(0)
        assignments.append({'ev_id': e['id'], 'charger_id': socket, 'rate_kw': rate})
    plan.update(assignments=assignments, tou_status='optimal' if result.success else 'feasible_time_limit',
                reason='Minimum tariff cost subject to known deadlines, site capacity and socket limits',
                planned_cost_upper_bound=float(np.dot(objective[:count], result.x[:count])),
                stability_policy={'override': 'ToU feasibility; preserve existing socket where possible'},
                assigned_since={e['id']: (previous or {}).get('assigned_since', {}).get(e['id'], now)
                                for e, _ in chosen})
    return plan

"""Read-only audit of saved models and the current DB; run before retraining."""
import json
import pickle
import sqlite3
from contextlib import closing
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
from sklearn.metrics import confusion_matrix
from ml_train import features

root=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
report=json.loads((root/'artifacts/ml_comparison.json').read_text())
with closing(sqlite3.connect(f'file:{(root/"station.db").as_posix()}?mode=ro',uri=True)) as conn:
    rows=conn.execute('SELECT day,hour,energy_kwh FROM demand_samples WHERE dataset=? ORDER BY day,hour',(report['dataset'],)).fetchall()
rows=[r for r in rows if r[0] in report['test_days']]
x=np.array([features(d,h) for d,h,y in rows]); y=np.array([r[2] for r in rows])
predictions={name:np.maximum(0,pickle.loads((root/'artifacts'/f'{name}.pkl').read_bytes()).predict(x)) for name in report['models']}
reference=next(iter(predictions.values()))
result={'test_positive_count':int((y>=30).sum()),'test_negative_count':int((y<30).sum()),
        'majority_baseline_accuracy':float(max((y>=30).mean(),(y<30).mean())),
        'models':{n:{'threshold_confusion_matrix':confusion_matrix(y>=30,p>=30,labels=[False,True]).tolist(),
                     'different_binary_predictions_vs_first':int(np.count_nonzero((p>=30)!=(reference>=30))),
                     'max_raw_prediction_difference_vs_first':float(abs(p-reference).max()),
                     'saved_inference_p95_ms':report['models'][n]['computational']['inference_p95_ms']}
                  for n,p in predictions.items()}}
(root/'artifacts/diagnosis_before.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2))
print(json.dumps(result,indent=2))

const pptxgen = require('pptxgenjs');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const root = path.join(__dirname, '..');
const read = name => JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, 'artifacts', name), 'utf8'));
const report = read('ml_comparison.json');
const live = read('live_check.json');
const tou = read('tou_comparison.json').results;
const ppt = new pptxgen();
ppt.layout = 'LAYOUT_WIDE';
ppt.author = 'GridSense Team 3';
ppt.title = 'GridSense - Distributed IoT EV Charging';
const modelResults = Object.entries(report.models).map(([name, m]) =>
  `${name}: RMSE ${m.test.rmse_kwh.toFixed(2)} kWh; F1 ${m.test.f1.toFixed(3)}`).join('\n');
const slides = [
  ['GridSense', 'Team 3: Smart EV Charging and Load Management\nTwo laptops, public cloud MQTT, Node-RED, SQLite and evaluated ML control'],
  ['Two-laptop architecture', 'Sensor laptop: Python sensor and actuator simulation\nTelemetry -> broker.emqx.io (TLS 8883) -> project Node-RED\nPath A: direct live dashboard\nPath B: SQLite commit -> database read -> ML -> decision\nCommands -> public broker -> sensor; acknowledgements -> project database'],
  ['Sensor and actuator', 'Six EVs; two charger sockets; 10 kW rated supply.\n1 kW base load; 9.5 kW total operating cap.\nReadings published every two seconds.\nActuator validates plans and applies them to simulated hardware.\nDuplicate, stale and unsafe commands are rejected.'],
  ['Five models, three categories', 'Traditional: decision tree and support vector models.\nEnsemble: random forest and XGBoost.\nNeural network: hidden layers 64 / 32 / 16.\nIndependent energy regressors and high-demand classifiers.\nAll training data is read from SQLite.'],
  ['Data and evaluation', `Real ACN-Data sessions (Caltech) with simulated SoC; synthetic fallback when offline.\nReport data source: ${report.data_source || 'unreported legacy source'}.\nHistory follows MQTT -> Node-RED -> SQLite.\n${report.training_days.length} training / ${report.validation_days.length} validation / ${report.test_days.length} test days.\nSelection uses validation RMSE and latency, never test scores.`],
  ['SoC and data provenance', 'Native current samples shape a calibrated SoC estimate.\nBattery capacity: OpenEV when identified, otherwise assumed 40 kWh.\nACN normally lacks vehicle identity and measured SoC.\nPrepared fleets let the sender keep only paho-mqtt.\nLee, Li and Low, ACN-Data, ACM e-Energy 2019.'],
  ['Measured model comparison', modelResults + '\nSelected: ' + report.selected_model],
  ['Performance measurements', 'Regression: RMSE, MAE and R2.\nIndependent high-demand classification: accuracy, precision, recall, F1.\nTraining time, warmed inference latency and serialized size.\nPeak Python-tracked allocations, not total process/GPU memory.\nClosed loop: acknowledgements, response time, switches and safety.'],
  ['Decision and automation', 'Feasibility-aware deadline scheduling with a 5% supply reserve.\nTariff optimization shifts flexible demand to cheaper periods.\nSolver failure falls back to deadline scheduling.\nFallback policy uses dwell time, socket stability and a deadband.\nModels load once at startup; restart after retraining.'],
  ['Verified local MQTT loop', `${live.history_rows} history rows; ${live.stored_cycles} live cycles.\n${live.accepted_commands} accepted commands; ${live.stored_acknowledgements} stored acknowledgements.\nDashboard HTTP ${live.dashboard_http}; peak ${live.max_observed_load_kw} kW.\nMean response ${live.mean_response_ms.toFixed(2)} ms; p95 ${live.p95_response_ms.toFixed(2)} ms.\nLocal test; actual two-laptop network timing remains separate.`],
  ['Tariff demonstration', `Same EVs, energy requirements and deadlines.\nDelivered energy: ${tou.tou.delivered_kwh} kWh; all deadlines met.\nDeadline-only cost: INR ${tou.edf.charging_cost_inr}.\nTariff-aware cost: INR ${tou.tou.charging_cost_inr}.\nPeak site load: ${tou.edf.peak_grid_kw} kW vs ${tou.tou.peak_grid_kw} kW.\nSynthetic evening scenario; not a general savings guarantee.`],
  ['Demonstration commands', 'Project laptop: ./start.ps1 -PublicBroker\nSensor laptop: python simulator.py --public-broker\nDashboard: http://127.0.0.1:1880/dashboard/station\nCopy mqtt-public.json to both laptops; observe readings and acknowledgements.\nUse --tou-demo on the sensor for the evening scenario.'],
  ['Limits and reproduction', 'ACN behavior when configured; derived SoC and simplified battery physics.\nCongested requests cannot all meet deadlines.\nML control-outcome improvement over baseline is not established.\nSynchronize laptop clocks for end-to-end latency measurements.\nPublic topics are not private; transmit no personal data (TLS enabled).\nSee README, GridSense_Final_Report.md and artifacts/*.json.']
];

FILE: node-red/build-public-flow.cjs
================================================
// Derive a public-broker flow without changing the local/private demo flow.
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const crypto = require('crypto');

function buildPublicFlow(profile, base) {
  const prefix = profile.topic_prefix;
  if (typeof prefix !== 'string' || !prefix || /^[\/]|[\/]$|[+#\x00]/.test(prefix)) {
    throw new Error('Invalid MQTT topic prefix');
  }
  const flows = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(base));
  const nodes = Object.fromEntries(flows.map(n => [n.id, n]));
  // New ID avoids inheriting any saved private-broker credentials.
  Object.assign(nodes.broker, {id: 'public-broker', name: 'EMQX public broker (TLS)',
    broker: profile.host, port: String(profile.port), usetls: true, tls: 'public-tls',
    clientid: 'gs_project_' + crypto.randomBytes(8).toString('hex')});
  delete nodes.broker.credentials;
  for (const node of flows) if (node.broker === 'broker') node.broker = 'public-broker';
  for (const [id, suffix] of [['sensor', 'telemetry'], ['actuator', 'actuator'], ['ack', 'ack']]) {
    nodes[id].topic = `${prefix}/${suffix}`;
  }
  flows.push({id: 'public-tls', type: 'tls-config', name: 'Verify EMQX certificate',
    cert: '', key: '', ca: '', certname: '', keyname: '', caname: '',
    servername: profile.host, verifyservercert: true, alpnprotocol: ''});
  nodes.station.label = 'GridSense public MQTT loop';
  nodes.station.info = `Public MQTT over verified TLS; topic prefix ${prefix}. Synthetic demo data only.`;
  return flows;
}

if (require.main === module) {
  const profile = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, '../mqtt-public.json'), 'utf8'));
  const base = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, 'flows.json'), 'utf8'));
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(__dirname, 'flows.public.json'), JSON.stringify(buildPublicFlow(profile, base), null, 2));
  console.log(`Public MQTT: ${profile.host}:${profile.port}; topics ${profile.topic_prefix}/*`);
}
module.exports = {buildPublicFlow};

// Local demonstration broker; replace with Mosquitto for deployment if desired.
const broker = require('aedes')();
const server = require('net').createServer(broker.handle);
const host = process.argv.includes('--lan') ? '0.0.0.0' : '127.0.0.1';
server.listen(1883, host, () => console.log(`MQTT demo broker on ${host}:1883`));

// Validate real flow Function code and compile Dashboard Vue templates locally.
const fs = require('fs');
const vm = require('vm');
const assert = require('assert/strict');
const {parse, compileScript, compileTemplate} = require('@vue/compiler-sfc');
const flows = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(__dirname + '/flows.json', 'utf8'));
const nodes = Object.fromEntries(flows.map(n=>[n.id,n]));
for (const name of ['station-overview','alerts','ml-comparison','price-load']) {
  const source=fs.readFileSync(`${__dirname}/templates/${name}.vue`,'utf8');
  const {descriptor,errors}=parse(source);
  assert.equal(errors.length,0);
  compileScript(descriptor,{id:name});
  const result=compileTemplate({source:descriptor.template.content,filename:name+'.vue',id:name});
  assert.deepEqual(result.errors,[]);
}
function run(id,payload,store={}) {
  const context={get:key=>store[key],set:(key,value)=>{store[key]=value}};
  const node={error:()=>{},warn:()=>{}};
  return vm.runInNewContext(`(function(){${nodes[id].func}})()`,{msg:{payload},context,node});
}
const p={kind:'telemetry',run_id:'test',sequence:1,timestamp:100,hour:16,
  load_kw:8,operating_limit_kw:9.5,unmanaged_demand_estimate_kw:15,
  price_per_kwh:12,charging_load_kw:7,vehicles:[{id:'EV1',soc:.55,target:.8,status:'charging'}]};
const bars=run('soc-router',p);
assert.equal(bars.length,6);
assert.ok(Math.abs(bars[0].payload-55)<1e-10);
assert.match(bars[1].ui_update.label,/unavailable/);
const store={};
const chart=run('load-series',p,store)[0];
assert.equal(chart.length,4); // reset and three series
assert.equal(chart[1].payload.x,100000);
assert.equal(run('load-series',p,store),null);
const history={};
for(let i=0;i<400;i++) run('price-history',{...p,sequence:i,hour:16+i/30},history);
assert.equal(history.history.points.length,360);
run('price-history',{...p,run_id:'new'},history);
assert.equal(history.history.points.length,1);
assert.equal(flows.filter(n=>n.type==='ui-progress').length,6);
assert.equal(nodes.sensor.topic,'ev/telemetry');
assert.equal(nodes.actuator.topic,'ev/actuator');
assert.equal(nodes.ack.topic,'ev/ack');
const ids=new Set(flows.map(n=>n.id));
for(const node of flows) for(const output of node.wires||[]) for(const id of output) assert.ok(ids.has(id),id);
console.log('Dashboard templates compile; SoC, chart mapping, deduplication, bounded history and wiring pass.');
const {buildPublicFlow} = require('./build-public-flow.cjs');
const profile = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(__dirname + '/../mqtt-public.json', 'utf8'));
const publicNodes = Object.fromEntries(buildPublicFlow(profile, flows).map(n => [n.id, n]));
assert.equal(publicNodes['public-broker'].usetls, true);
assert.equal(publicNodes['public-tls'].verifyservercert, true);
assert.equal(publicNodes['public-tls'].servername, profile.host);
for (const [id, suffix] of [['sensor','telemetry'], ['actuator','actuator'], ['ack','ack']]) {
  assert.equal(publicNodes[id].topic, `${profile.topic_prefix}/${suffix}`);
  assert.equal(publicNodes[id].broker, 'public-broker');
}
assert.equal(nodes.sensor.topic, 'ev/telemetry'); // Base flow remains unchanged.
assert.notEqual(buildPublicFlow(profile, flows).find(n=>n.id==='public-broker').clientid, publicNodes['public-broker'].clientid);
console.log('Public profile: matching topics, verified TLS, unique client IDs and local-flow isolation pass.');

FILE: node-red/templates/alerts.vue

<template>
  <section class="gs-alerts" aria-live="polite">
    <div class="gs-section-heading"><div><p class="gs-eyebrow">REQUIRES YOUR ATTENTION</p><h2>Station health</h2></div><span v-if="msg?.payload?.decision" class="gs-muted">{{ msg.payload.decision.alerts?.length || 0 }} active alerts</span></div>
    <p v-if="!msg?.payload?.decision">Waiting for analytics...</p>
    <template v-else>
      <div v-if="stale" class="gs-alert warning">Warning: analytics is stale. Last decisions are shown below.</div>
      <div v-for="(alert, i) in alerts" :key="i" :class="['gs-alert', alert.level]">
        <strong>{{ alert.level.toUpperCase() }}</strong> â€” {{ alert.text }}
      </div>
      <p class="gs-muted">{{ policyLabel }}</p>
    </template>
  </section>
</template>
<script>
export default {
  data() { return { now: Date.now(), timer: null } },
  mounted() { this.timer = setInterval(() => { this.now = Date.now() }, 1000) },
  unmounted() { clearInterval(this.timer) },
  computed: {
    policyLabel() { return {optimal:'Price optimization active Â· deadline constraints satisfied by the plan',feasible_time_limit:'Feasible price plan Â· optimization time limit reached',idle:'No charging demand to schedule',fallback_edf:'Deadline priority active Â· price optimization unavailable',disabled:'Price optimization is turned off'}[this.msg?.payload?.decision?.tou_status] || 'Waiting for scheduling status' },
    stale() { return this.now - (this.msg?.payload?.decision?.sensor_timestamp || 0) * 1000 > 10000 },
    alerts() {
      const values = this.msg?.payload?.decision?.alerts || [];
      return values.length ? values.map(text => ({ text,
        level: /cannot meet|unmet|exceeded|violation/i.test(text) ? 'critical' : 'warning'
      })) : this.stale ? [] : [{ level: 'success', text: 'No current scheduling alerts. Charging is being managed automatically.' }];
    }
  }
}
</script>
<style>
.gs-alerts { padding: 16px; }
.gs-alert { padding: 12px 16px; margin: 8px 0; border-radius: 8px; border-left: 5px solid; }
.gs-alert.critical { background: #fee2e2; color: #991b1b; border-color: #dc2626; }
.gs-alert.warning { background: #fef3c7; color: #854d0e; border-color: #d97706; }
.gs-alert.success { background: #dcfce7; color: #166534; border-color: #16a34a; }
</style>

    </div>
    <div style="overflow-x:auto">
      <table>
        <thead><tr><th>Model</th><th>Accuracy</th><th>RMSE (kWh)</th><th>MAE (kWh)</th><th>RÂ²</th><th>p95 inference (ms)</th></tr></thead>
        <tbody><tr v-for="(m, name) in msg.payload.decision.performance.models" :key="name"
          :class="{selected: name === msg.payload.decision.model}">
          <td><strong>{{ modelName(name) }}</strong> <span v-if="name === msg.payload.decision.model" class="gs-model-winner">SELECTED</span></td>
          <td>{{ fmt(m.test.accuracy * 100, 1) }}%</td><td>{{ fmt(m.test.rmse_kwh) }}</td>
          <td>{{ fmt(m.test.mae_kwh) }}</td><td>{{ fmt(m.test.r2, 3) }}</td>
          <td>{{ fmt(m.computational.inference_p95_ms, 3) }}</td>
        </tr></tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
    <p v-if="!Object.keys(msg.payload.decision.performance.models).length">Train models to populate this table.</p>
    <p class="gs-ml-note">Selected by validation error, with a 100 ms p95 latency limit. Lower RMSE and MAE are better; higher RÂ² and accuracy are better.</p>
    <details><summary>How to read these metrics</summary><p>Accuracy measures the companion high-demand classifier (energy â‰¥30 kWh). RMSE, MAE and RÂ² measure the energy regressor. Test scores use held-out days from {{ msg.payload.decision.performance.data_source || 'unreported legacy source' }}. The selected model must beat the constant baseline on validation RMSE.</p></details>
    <details><summary>Closed-loop performance</summary>
      <div class="gs-control-grid"><div v-for="(value, key) in msg.payload.closed_loop" :key="key"><span>{{ metricName(key) }}</span><strong>{{ value === null ? 'Not yet measurable' : typeof value==='number' ? fmt(value,Number.isInteger(value)?0:3) : value }}</strong></div></div>
    </details>
    <details><summary>Current charging assignments</summary>
      <p v-for="a in msg.payload.decision.assignments" :key="a.charger_id">{{ a.charger_id }} â†’ {{ a.ev_id }}: {{ fmt(a.rate_kw) }} kW</p>
    </details>
  </section><p v-else>Waiting for stored-data analytics...</p>
</template>
<script>
export default {
  methods: {
    fmt(v, digits=2) { return Number.isFinite(v) ? v.toFixed(digits) : 'â€”' },
    modelName(name) { return {traditional_decision_tree:'Decision Tree',ensemble_random_forest:'Random Forest',deep_ann:'Neural Network',traditional_svr:'Support Vector',ensemble_xgboost:'XGBoost',constant_baseline:'Constant baseline',untrained_safe_rules:'Deadline rules (untrained)'}[name] || name },
    metricName(key) { const value=key.replace(/_/g,' ').replace(/\bkw\b/g,'kW').replace(/\bkwh\b/g,'kWh'); return value.charAt(0).toUpperCase()+value.slice(1) }
  },
  computed: { forecast() {
    const d = this.msg?.payload?.decision;
    return d?.model === 'untrained_safe_rules' ? 'Unavailable (untrained)' : this.fmt(d?.forecast_next_hour_kwh) + ' kWh';
  } }
}
</script>

<template>
  <section class="gs-price-chart" style="padding:16px">
    <div class="gs-section-heading"><div><p class="gs-eyebrow">TIME-OF-USE INSIGHTS</p><h2>Charge when energy costs less</h2></div>
      <label class="gs-chart-range">Show <select v-model.number="windowSize" aria-label="Price chart time window"><option :value="60">Last 2 simulated hours</option><option :value="180">Last 6 simulated hours</option><option :value="360">Last 12 simulated hours</option></select></label></div>
    <p class="gs-chart-legend"><span style="color:#166534">● Charging load (kW), left axis</span> ·
       <span style="color:#9a3412">● Price (INR/kWh), right axis</span></p>
    <svg v-if="points.length" viewBox="0 0 800 300" role="img" aria-label="Dual-axis line chart of charging load and electricity price over simulated time" style="width:100%;min-height:220px">
      <g v-for="tick in [0,1,2,3,4]" :key="tick">
        <line x1="65" x2="730" :y1="250-tick*50" :y2="250-tick*50" stroke="#e2e8f0"/>
        <text x="55" :y="255-tick*50" text-anchor="end" fill="#166534" font-size="13">{{ (loadMax*tick/4).toFixed(1) }}</text>
        <text x="740" :y="255-tick*50" fill="#9a3412" font-size="13">{{ (priceMax*tick/4).toFixed(1) }}</text>
      </g>
      <text x="65" y="25" fill="#166534">kW</text><text x="675" y="25" fill="#9a3412">INR/kWh</text>
      <polyline :points="line('load',loadMax)" fill="none" stroke="#166534" stroke-width="3"/>
      <polyline :points="line('price',priceMax)" fill="none" stroke="#c2410c" stroke-width="2"/>
      <text x="65" y="277" font-size="13">{{ points[0].hour.toFixed(2) }}</text>
      <text x="730" y="277" text-anchor="end" font-size="13">{{ points[points.length-1].hour.toFixed(2) }}</text>
      <text x="400" y="295" text-anchor="middle" font-size="13">Simulated hour (absolute, across midnight)</text>
    </svg>
    <p v-else>Waiting for telemetry with tariff fields...</p>
    <p v-if="latest" class="gs-chart-current"><span>Latest reading</span><strong>{{ latest.load.toFixed(2) }} kW</strong><strong>{{ latest.price.toFixed(2) }} INR/kWh</strong></p>
    <small class="gs-muted">{{ points.length }} samples shown · Charging load excludes base load · Simulated tariff</small>
  </section>
</template>
<script>
export default {
  data() { return {windowSize:180} },
  computed: {
    points() { const all=this.msg?.payload?.points || []; const end=all[all.length-1]?.hour; return all.filter(p=>p.hour>=end-this.windowSize/30) },
    latest() { return this.points[this.points.length-1] },
    loadMax() { return Math.max(1, ...this.points.map(p=>p.load)) * 1.1 },
    priceMax() { return Math.max(1, ...this.points.map(p=>p.price)) * 1.1 }
  },
  methods: {
    line(key, max) {
      const start=this.points[0].hour;
      const span=Math.max(1/30, this.points[this.points.length-1].hour-start);
      return this.points.map(p => `${65+665*(p.hour-start)/span},${250-200*p[key]/max}`).join(' ');
    }
  }
}
</script>

<template>
  <section class="gs-overview">
    <header class="gs-hero">

        <span>Last sensor update · {{ lastSeen }}</span>

      </div>

      <div class="gs-kpis">
        <article class="gs-kpi gs-load-card"><p class="gs-label">Grid load</p>

          <p class="gs-muted">{{ nextDeparture ? nextDeparture.id + ' · ' + fmt(nextDeparture.remaining_kwh) + ' kWh remaining' : 'No upcoming departures' }}</p></article>
      </div>

      </div>

      <div v-for="ev in visibleVehicles" :key="ev.id" class="gs-fleet-row">
        <div class="gs-vehicle"><span class="gs-vehicle-icon" aria-hidden="true">↯</span><div><strong>{{ ev.id }}</strong><span :class="['gs-status', ev.status]">{{ statusLabel(ev.status) }}</span></div></div>

      <p class="gs-footnote">Synthetic station data · {{ state.capacity_kw }} kW physical rating · 5% capacity reserve</p>

<script>
export default {
  data() { return {now:Date.now(),timer:null,search:'',filter:'all',filters:[{key:'all',label:'All sessions'},{key:'active',label:'On site'},{key:'queued',label:'Waiting'},{key:'complete',label:'Complete'},{key:'departed',label:'Departed'}]} },
  mounted() { this.timer=setInterval(()=>{this.now=Date.now()},1000) },
  unmounted() { clearInterval(this.timer) },
  computed: {
    state() { const p=this.msg?.payload; return Array.isArray(p?.vehicles) ? p : null },
    vehicles() { return this.state?.vehicles || [] },
    fresh() { const age=this.now-(this.state?.timestamp || 0)*1000; return !!this.state && age>=-5000 && age<10000 },
    lastSeen() { return Number.isFinite(this.state?.timestamp) ? new Date(this.state.timestamp*1000).toLocaleTimeString() : 'Not received' },
    charging() { return this.vehicles.filter(e=>e.status==='charging').length },
    queued() { return this.vehicles.filter(e=>e.status==='queued').length },
    completed() { return this.vehicles.filter(e=>e.status==='complete').length },
    gauge() {
      const kw=this.state?.load_kw;
      if(!Number.isFinite(kw) || kw<0) return {value:'—',percent:0,color:'#64748b',label:'Reading unavailable'};
      return {value:kw.toFixed(2),percent:Math.min(100,kw*10),
        color:kw<7?'#16a34a':kw<=9.5?'#eab308':'#dc2626',
        label:kw<7?'Normal load':kw<=9.5?'High load':kw<10?'Above operating limit':'At or above rated capacity'};
    },
    overLimit() { return this.state?.load_kw > this.state?.operating_limit_kw+1e-6 },
    tariffLabel() { const p=this.state?.price_per_kwh; return !Number.isFinite(p)?'Tariff unavailable':p<=3?'Off-peak rate':p>=12?'Peak rate':'Standard rate' },
    nextDeparture() { return this.vehicles.filter(e=>e.departure>this.state.hour && e.arrival<=this.state.hour && e.remaining_kwh>.01).sort((a,b)=>a.departure-b.departure)[0] },
    visibleVehicles() { return this.vehicles.filter(e=>e.id.toLowerCase().includes(this.search.toLowerCase()) && (this.filter==='all' || (this.filter==='active' ? e.arrival<=this.state.hour && this.state.hour<e.departure : e.status===this.filter))) }
  },
  methods: {
    fmt(value,digits=2) { return Number.isFinite(value)?value.toFixed(digits):'—' },
    simTime(hour) { if(!Number.isFinite(hour)) return '—'; const minutes=Math.floor(hour*60+1e-6); const day=Math.floor(minutes/1440); return (day?'Day '+(day+1)+' · ':'')+String(Math.floor(minutes/60)%24).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(minutes%60).padStart(2,'0') },
    statusLabel(status) { return {scheduled:'Scheduled',queued:'Waiting',charging:'Charging',connected:'Connected',complete:'Target reached',departed:'Departed'}[status] || status }
  }
}
</script>

"""Actuator driver co-located with physical simulator; subscribes to ev/actuator.

It shares the simulated hardware state with simulator.py, so there is only one
authoritative station. It does not make scheduling decisions.
"""
import json
import time


class Actuator:
    def __init__(self, station, run_id, lock, topic_prefix='ev'):
        self.station, self.run_id, self.lock = station, run_id, lock
        self.command_topic = topic_prefix + '/actuator'
        self.ack_topic = topic_prefix + '/ack'
        self.switch_count = 0
        self.rapid_switch_count = 0
        self.last_switch = {}

    def on_connect(self, client, userdata, flags, reason_code, properties):
        if reason_code == 0:
            client.subscribe(self.command_topic, qos=1)

    def on_message(self, client, userdata, msg):
        try:
            command = json.loads(msg.payload)
            if not isinstance(command, dict) or command.get('run_id') != self.run_id:
                return
            with self.lock:
                before = {e.charger:e.id for e in self.station.vehicles if e.charger and e.power_kw > 0}
                accepted = command.get('run_id') == self.run_id and self.station.accept(command)
                if accepted:
                    after = {e.charger:e.id for e in self.station.vehicles if e.charger and e.power_kw > 0}
                    for charger in self.station.chargers:
                        if before.get(charger) != after.get(charger):
                            now = self.station.hour
                            if charger in self.last_switch and now-self.last_switch[charger] < .1-1e-8:
                                self.rapid_switch_count += 1
                            self.last_switch[charger] = now
                            self.switch_count += 1
            client.publish(self.ack_topic,json.dumps({'run_id':self.run_id,'sequence':command.get('sequence'),
                'accepted':accepted, 'received_at':time.time(),
                'response_ms':max(0,(time.time()-command['sensor_timestamp'])*1000),
                'inference_ms':command.get('inference_ms'),
                'decision_ms':command.get('decision_ms'),
                'switch_count':self.switch_count,'rapid_switch_count':self.rapid_switch_count}),qos=1)
        except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as exc:
            print('Rejected actuator message:',exc,flush=True)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit('Actuator is attached to the sensor/plant process. Run python simulator.py.')
