"""Physical sensor layer + simulated plant. Publishes only through MQTT.

--history replays synthetic historical sensor observations through ev/telemetry;
Node-RED persists them. This script never writes training data directly to DB.
"""
import argparse
import json
import os
import ssl
import getpass
import threading
import time
import uuid
import logging
from pathlib import Path
import paho.mqtt.client as mqtt
try:
    from .station_core import Station, real_or_synthetic_history, real_or_synthetic_vehicles, tou_demo_station
    from .actuator import Actuator
    from .tariff import telemetry_fields
except ImportError:
    from station_core import Station, real_or_synthetic_history, real_or_synthetic_vehicles, tou_demo_station
    from actuator import Actuator
    from tariff import telemetry_fields


def history_payload(synthetic_only=False):
    rows = real_or_synthetic_history(days=42, prefer_real=not synthetic_only)
    source = rows[0].get('source', 'synthetic-fallback') if rows else 'synthetic-fallback'
    days = max((r['day'] for r in rows), default=41)+1
    bins = [[0.0]*24 for _ in range(days)]
    for r in rows:
        bins[r['day']][r['hour']] += r['energy_kwh']
    return {'kind':'history','dataset':'acn-caltech-v1' if source == 'acn-data-caltech' else 'synthetic-history-v5','source':source,
            'samples':[{'day':d,'hour':h,'energy_kwh':value}
                       for d, row in enumerate(bins) for h,value in enumerate(row)]}


def configure_connection(client, args):
    if args.ca_cert and not args.tls:
        raise ValueError('--ca-cert requires --tls')
    if args.username and not args.tls:
        raise ValueError('Use --tls when sending MQTT login credentials')
    if args.tls:
        client.tls_set_context(ssl.create_default_context(cafile=args.ca_cert))
    if args.username:
        password = os.environ.get('MQTT_PASSWORD')
        if password is None:
            password = getpass.getpass('MQTT password: ')
        client.username_pw_set(args.username, password)


def apply_public_profile(args):
    profile = json.loads(Path(__file__).with_name('mqtt-public.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    args.broker_host = profile['host']
    args.broker_port = profile['port']
    args.topic_prefix = profile['topic_prefix']
    args.tls = True
    # Public mode must never forward credentials intended for a private broker.
    args.username = None


def validate_topic_prefix(prefix):
    if not prefix or prefix.startswith('/') or prefix.endswith('/') or any(c in prefix for c in ('+', '#', '\x00')):
        raise ValueError('Topic prefix must be nonempty, without wildcards or leading/trailing slash')
    return prefix


def main():
    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format='%(levelname)s %(message)s')
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument('--synthetic-only', action='store_true', help='Force deterministic offline data; do not fetch or use prepared ACN data')
    parser.add_argument('--public-broker', action='store_true', help='Use the shared mqtt-public.json profile with verified TLS and no credentials')
    parser.add_argument('--topic-prefix', default='ev', help='Shared MQTT topic prefix (default: ev for local/private mode)')
    parser.add_argument('--broker-host', default='127.0.0.1', help='MQTT broker address (project laptop LAN IP for two-laptop mode)')
    parser.add_argument('--broker-port', type=int, help='MQTT TCP port (default: 8883 with TLS, otherwise 1883)')
    parser.add_argument('--tls', action='store_true', help='Verify the cloud broker certificate and encrypt MQTT traffic')
    parser.add_argument('--ca-cert', help='Optional broker CA certificate PEM file')
    parser.add_argument('--username', default=os.environ.get('MQTT_USERNAME'), help='MQTT username; password is prompted or read from MQTT_PASSWORD')
    parser.add_argument('--history',action='store_true',help='Replay 42 historical days via MQTT for database training')
    parser.add_argument('--tou-demo', action='store_true', help='Feasible 16:00â€“24:00 tariff-shifting demo')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    try:
        if args.public_broker:
            apply_public_profile(args)
        validate_topic_prefix(args.topic_prefix)
    except (ValueError, OSError, KeyError) as exc:
        parser.error(str(exc))
    telemetry_topic = args.topic_prefix + '/telemetry'
    # Fetch history before opening MQTT so slow paginated downloads cannot leave
    # a partially initialized sensor connected. History mode never fetches vehicles twice.
    history = history_payload(args.synthetic_only) if args.history else None
    if args.tou_demo:
        station = tou_demo_station()
        station.data_source = 'synthetic-tou-demo'
    elif args.history:
        station = Station()
    else:
        station = Station(real_or_synthetic_vehicles(prefer_real=not args.synthetic_only))
        station.data_source = getattr(station.vehicles[0], 'data_source', 'synthetic-fallback')
        if station.data_source == 'acn-data-caltech':
            station.hour = min(v.arrival for v in station.vehicles)
            station.refresh()
    lock = threading.Lock()
    # The hardware rating remains 10 kW; Station enforces a 9.5 kW operating
    # ceiling including base load, independently of received command limits.
    run_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    client = mqtt.Client(mqtt.CallbackAPIVersion.VERSION2,client_id='gridsense_sensor_'+run_id[:8])
    try:
        configure_connection(client, args)
    except ValueError as exc:
        parser.error(str(exc))
    actuator = Actuator(station,run_id,lock,topic_prefix=args.topic_prefix)
    connected = threading.Event()
    connection_errors = []
    def on_connect(client, userdata, flags, reason_code, properties):
        if reason_code != 0:
            connection_errors.append(str(reason_code))
        else:
            actuator.on_connect(client, userdata, flags, reason_code, properties)
        connected.set()
    client.on_connect = on_connect
    client.on_message = actuator.on_message
    client.connect(args.broker_host,args.broker_port or (8883 if args.tls else 1883),60)
    client.loop_start()
    try:
        if not connected.wait(15):
            raise RuntimeError('MQTT connection acknowledgement timed out; check the broker address, TLS and network')
        if connection_errors:
            raise RuntimeError('MQTT connection rejected: ' + connection_errors[0])
        print(f'Connected to {args.broker_host}; publishing {telemetry_topic} and listening on {args.topic_prefix}/actuator.', flush=True)
        if args.history:
            receipt = client.publish(telemetry_topic,json.dumps(history),qos=1)
            receipt.wait_for_publish(timeout=10)
            if not receipt.is_published():
                raise RuntimeError('Broker did not acknowledge history publish')
            print(f"Published {len(history['samples'])} hourly observations; source={history['source']}. Check /health before training.")
            return
        while True:
            with lock:
                state = station.snapshot()
                state.update(telemetry_fields(state))
                state.update(kind='telemetry',run_id=run_id,timestamp=time.time())
            client.publish(telemetry_topic,json.dumps(state),qos=1)
            time.sleep(2)
            with lock:
                station.step(1/30)
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        pass
    finally:
        client.disconnect()
        client.loop_stop()


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()



================================================
FILE: sender/station_core.py

"""Deterministic EV station, deadline scheduler and demand analytics (no I/O)."""
import math
import random
import logging
import json
from pathlib import Path
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict

try:
    import acn_data
except ImportError:
    acn_data = None

LOG = logging.getLogger(__name__)

SAFETY_FACTOR = 0.95


def site_limit_kw(state):
    """Rated site capacity remains visible; 5% is reserved from all EV plans."""
    return max(0.0, state['capacity_kw'] * SAFETY_FACTOR)


def edf_key(ev, now):
    # Do not let an already impossible request starve a still-feasible deadline.
    impossible = ev['remaining_kwh'] > max(0, ev['departure']-now)*ev['max_kw'] + 1e-6
    return (impossible, ev['departure'], ev['arrival'], ev['id'])


@dataclass
class Vehicle:
    id: str
    arrival: float
    departure: float
    capacity: float
    soc: float
    target: float
    max_kw: float = 7.0
    charger: str | None = None
    status: str = "scheduled"
    power_kw: float = 0.0
    delivered_kwh: float = 0.0

    @property
    def remaining(self):
        return max(0.0, (self.target - self.soc) * self.capacity)


def demo_vehicles():
    # Explicitly synthetic; more vehicles than sockets demonstrates queuing.
    return [Vehicle("EV1", 0, 3, 40, .40, .70),
            Vehicle("EV2", 0, 2, 40, .50, .90),
            Vehicle("EV3", .25, 4, 50, .40, .65),
            Vehicle("EV4", .5, 2.5, 30, .60, .80),
            Vehicle("EV5", 1, 5, 60, .50, .70),
            Vehicle("EV6", 2, 6, 40, .30, .60)]


class Station:
    def __init__(self, vehicles=None, chargers=2, capacity_kw=10.0):
        self.vehicles = demo_vehicles() if vehicles is None else vehicles
        self.chargers = [f"C{i+1}" for i in range(chargers)]
        self.capacity_kw = capacity_kw
        self.base_kw = 1.0
        self.hour = 0.0
        self.sequence = 0
        self.command_sequence = -1
        self.command = {}
        self.command_until = -1.0
        self.last_applied_sequence = -1
        self.refresh()

    def refresh(self):
        for ev in self.vehicles:
            if self.hour >= ev.departure:
                ev.status = "departed"
                ev.charger = None
                ev.power_kw = 0
            elif ev.remaining < 1e-8:
                ev.status = "complete"
                ev.charger = None
                ev.power_kw = 0
            elif self.hour >= ev.arrival and ev.charger is None:
                ev.status = "queued"

    def snapshot(self):
        self.refresh()
        rows = []
        for ev in self.vehicles:
            row = asdict(ev)
            row["remaining_kwh"] = round(ev.remaining, 6)
            rows.append(row)
        return {"sequence": self.sequence, "hour": self.hour,
                "capacity_kw": self.capacity_kw, "base_kw": self.base_kw,
                "operating_limit_kw": self.capacity_kw * SAFETY_FACTOR,
                "chargers": self.chargers, "vehicles": rows,
                "load_kw": self.base_kw + sum(e.power_kw for e in self.vehicles),
                "applied_sequence": self.last_applied_sequence,
                "source": getattr(self, 'data_source', 'synthetic-fallback')}

    def accept(self, command):
        # A whole-station plan is validated before any actuator state changes.
        seq = command["sequence"]
        if not isinstance(seq, int) or seq <= self.command_sequence or seq != self.sequence:
            return False
        assignments = command["assignments"]
        seen_ev, seen_charger = set(), set()
        total = 0.0
        by_id = {e.id: e for e in self.vehicles}
        for a in assignments:
            ev = by_id.get(a["ev_id"])
            rate = float(a["rate_kw"])
            if (ev is None or a["charger_id"] not in self.chargers
                    or ev.id in seen_ev or a["charger_id"] in seen_charger
                    or not math.isfinite(rate) or rate < 0 or rate > ev.max_kw
                    or not ev.arrival <= self.hour < ev.departure or ev.remaining <= 0):
                return False
            seen_ev.add(ev.id)
            seen_charger.add(a["charger_id"])
            total += rate
        if total > max(0, self.capacity_kw * SAFETY_FACTOR - self.base_kw) + 1e-9:
            return False
        self.command = {a["ev_id"]: dict(a) for a in assignments}
        self.command_sequence = seq
        self.last_applied_sequence = seq
        self.command_until = self.hour + 0.1
        for ev in self.vehicles:
            a = self.command.get(ev.id)
            ev.charger = a["charger_id"] if a else None
            ev.power_kw = a["rate_kw"] if a else 0.0
            if a:
                ev.status = "charging" if ev.power_kw > 0 else "connected"
        self.refresh()
        return True

    def step(self, hours):
        if not math.isfinite(hours) or hours <= 0:
            raise ValueError("hours must be positive and finite")
        # Clamp locally even if supply changes after a command was accepted.
        total = sum(a["rate_kw"] for a in self.command.values())
        scale = min(1, max(0, self.capacity_kw*SAFETY_FACTOR-self.base_kw) / total) if total else 0
        for ev in self.vehicles:
            a = self.command.get(ev.id)
            duration = max(0, min(hours, ev.departure-self.hour,
                                  self.command_until-self.hour))
            if a and duration > 0 and ev.arrival <= self.hour and ev.remaining > 0:
                taper = min(1, max(0, (1-ev.soc)/.2))
                power = min(ev.max_kw, a["rate_kw"]) * scale * taper
                energy = min(ev.remaining, power * duration)
                ev.soc += energy / ev.capacity
                ev.delivered_kwh += energy
                ev.power_kw = energy / hours
            else:
                ev.power_kw = 0
            if ev.charger is not None:
                ev.status = "charging" if ev.power_kw > 0 else "connected"
        self.hour += hours
        self.sequence += 1
        self.refresh()


def tou_demo_station(unmanaged=False):
    """Feasible peak-to-off-peak scenario; separate from the overloaded original demo."""
    station = Station([
        Vehicle('EV1', 16, 18, 40, .3, .4),
        Vehicle('EV2', 16, 24, 40, .3, .6),
    ], capacity_kw=100 if unmanaged else 10)
    station.hour = 16.
    return station


def historical_sessions(days=28, seed=42):
    """Independent synthetic training days with morning/evening arrival peaks."""
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    rows = []
    for day in range(days):
        for hour in range(24):
            count = max(0, round((4 if hour in (8, 9, 17, 18) else 1) + rng.gauss(0, 1)))
            for _ in range(count):
                rows.append({"day": day, "hour": hour,
                             "energy_kwh": rng.uniform(5, 20)})
    return rows


def real_or_synthetic_history(days=42, prefer_real=True):
    if prefer_real and acn_data is not None:
        try:
            if acn_data.LOCAL_DIR.exists():
                sessions = acn_data.saved_sessions(for_history=True, days=days)
            else:
                start, end = acn_data.recent_window(days)
                sessions = acn_data.fetch_sessions(start=start, end=end)
            rows = acn_data.to_demand_samples(sessions)
            active_days = {r['day'] for r in rows}
            if len(active_days) < 18:
                raise ValueError('fewer than 18 days with observed sessions')
            # The API query covers complete local days. Missing hourly arrivals
            # inside that fully fetched interval are observed zeros, not fake sessions.
            bins = {(r['day'], r['hour']): r['energy_kwh'] for r in rows}
            dense = [{'dataset': 'acn-caltech-v1', 'day': d, 'hour': h,
                      'energy_kwh': bins.get((d, h), 0.0), 'source': 'acn-data-caltech'}
                     for d in range(max(active_days)+1) for h in range(24)]
            LOG.warning('Using ACN-Data history: %d sessions, %d complete hourly days', len(sessions), len(dense)//24)
            # Export only derived fleet parameters for a paho-only sensor laptop.
            fleet = acn_data.to_vehicle_sessions(sessions)
            if fleet:
                target = Path(__file__).with_name('acn_vehicles.json')
                target.write_text(json.dumps({'source': 'acn-data-caltech', 'vehicles': [asdict(v) for v in fleet]}), encoding='utf-8')
            return dense
        except Exception as exc:
            # Never include provider exception text, URLs or credentials in logs.
            LOG.warning('[FALLBACK] Using synthetic history (%s)', type(exc).__name__)
    else:
        LOG.warning('[FALLBACK] Using synthetic history (%s)', 'forced offline' if not prefer_real else 'ACN module unavailable')
    return historical_sessions(days, seed=42)


def real_or_synthetic_vehicles(prefer_real=True):
    if prefer_real and acn_data is not None:
        try:
            if acn_data.LOCAL_DIR.exists():
                sessions = acn_data.saved_sessions()
            else:
                start, end = acn_data.recent_window(7)
                sessions = acn_data.fetch_sessions(start=start, end=end)
            fleet = acn_data.to_vehicle_sessions(sessions)
            if fleet:
                return fleet[:6]
            raise ValueError('No usable ACN vehicles')
        except Exception as exc:
            LOG.warning('[FALLBACK] Using synthetic vehicles (%s)', type(exc).__name__)
    elif prefer_real:
        # A copied sender/ folder has no data-access packages or token. It can
        # replay the explicitly exported fleet from the project laptop instead.
        try:
            payload = json.loads(Path(__file__).with_name('acn_vehicles.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
            fleet = [Vehicle(**row) for row in payload['vehicles'][:6]]
            if payload.get('source') != 'acn-data-caltech' or not fleet:
                raise ValueError('Invalid prepared fleet')
            for vehicle in fleet:
                if not (vehicle.departure > vehicle.arrival and vehicle.capacity > 0 and 0 <= vehicle.soc <= vehicle.target <= 1 and 0 < vehicle.max_kw <= 7):
                    raise ValueError('Invalid prepared vehicle')
                vehicle.data_source = 'acn-data-caltech'
            return fleet
        except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
            LOG.warning('[FALLBACK] Using synthetic vehicles (no prepared ACN fleet)')
    else:
        LOG.warning('[FALLBACK] Using synthetic vehicles (forced offline)')
    return demo_vehicles()


def demand_model():
    """Hourly seasonal-mean regression, evaluated on held-out whole days."""
    rows = historical_sessions()
    bins = [[0.0]*24 for _ in range(28)]
    counts = [0]*24
    for r in rows:
        bins[r["day"]][r["hour"]] += r["energy_kwh"]
        counts[r["hour"]] += 1
    profile = [sum(d[h] for d in bins[:21])/21 for h in range(24)]
    baseline = sum(profile)/24
    errors = [abs(day[h]-profile[h]) for day in bins[21:] for h in range(24)]
    naive = [abs(day[h]-baseline) for day in bins[21:] for h in range(24)]
    return {"profile_kwh": profile, "arrivals_per_hour": [n/28 for n in counts],
            "mae_kwh": sum(errors)/len(errors), "baseline_mae_kwh": sum(naive)/len(naive),
            "training_days": 21, "test_days": 7,
            "source": "seeded synthetic history; not a real-world accuracy claim"}


def schedule(state, model=None):
    model = demand_model() if model is None else model
    now = state["hour"]
    active = [e for e in state["vehicles"]
              if e["arrival"] <= now < e["departure"] and e["remaining_kwh"] > 1e-8]
    active.sort(key=lambda e: edf_key(e, now))
    available = max(0.0, site_limit_kw(state)-state["base_kw"])
    forecast = model["profile_kwh"][int(now+1) % 24]
    typical = sum(model["profile_kwh"])/24
    # Forecasted busy periods cause feasible sessions to charge earlier.
    reserve_factor = 1.25 if forecast > typical else 1.0
    assignments, alerts = [], []
    for e in active:
        if e["remaining_kwh"] > (e["departure"]-now)*e["max_kw"] + 1e-6:
            alerts.append(f'{e["id"]}: cannot meet target before departure at maximum rate')
    for deadline in sorted({e["departure"] for e in active}):
        needed = sum(e["remaining_kwh"] for e in active if e["departure"] <= deadline)
        if needed > available*(deadline-now) + 1e-6:
            alerts.append("Shared supply cannot meet all current deadlines")
            break
    for charger, e in zip(state["chargers"], active):
        # Work-conserving EDF: finish the first feasible deadline before
        # spreading scarce power across later requests.
        rate = min(e["max_kw"], available)
        rate = math.floor(max(0, rate)*100)/100
        available -= rate
        assignments.append({"charger_id": charger, "ev_id": e["id"], "rate_kw": rate})
    for e in state["vehicles"]:
        if e["status"] == "departed" and e["remaining_kwh"] > .01:
            alerts.append(f'{e["id"]}: departed with {e["remaining_kwh"]:.2f} kWh unmet')
    return {"sequence": state["sequence"], "assignments": assignments,
            "alerts": alerts, "forecast_next_hour_kwh": forecast,
            "forecast_factor": reserve_factor, "analytics": model,
            "operating_limit_kw": site_limit_kw(state),
            "reason": "Feasibility-aware EDF; impossible requests get best effort; 5% site reserve"}



================================================
FILE: sender/tariff.py
================================================
"""Illustrative tariff in INR/kWh; hours are absolute SIMULATION hours."""
import math

HOURLY_PRICES = (3.,) * 6 + (6.,) * 10 + (12.,) * 6 + (3.,) * 2


def price_at(hour):
    if not math.isfinite(hour):
        raise ValueError('Simulation hour must be finite')
    return HOURLY_PRICES[math.floor(hour) % 24]


def telemetry_fields(state):
    active = [e for e in state['vehicles'] if e['arrival'] <= state['hour'] < e['departure']
              and e['remaining_kwh'] > 1e-8]
    # Demand envelope only: not a separate counterfactual simulation.
    demand = sum(sorted((e['max_kw'] for e in active), reverse=True)[:len(state['chargers'])])
    return {'price_per_kwh': price_at(state['hour']), 'price_currency': 'INR',
            'charging_load_kw': max(0., state['load_kw'] - state['base_kw']),
            'unmanaged_demand_estimate_kw': state['base_kw'] + demand}

"""Owns a complete local test stack; ports 1880/1883/8765 must be free."""
import json
import argparse
import os
import socket
import sqlite3
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path
import paho.mqtt.client as mqtt

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
parser.add_argument('--synthetic-only', action='store_true')
parser.add_argument('--mock-acn', action='store_true', help='Exercise real-source processing with deterministic ACN fixtures, no external data access')
args = parser.parse_args()
synthetic_only = args.synthetic_only or (not args.mock_acn and not os.environ.get('ACN_DATA_TOKEN'))
sensor_script = 'tests/mock_acn_sender.py' if args.mock_acn else 'simulator.py'
sensor_flags = ['--synthetic-only'] if synthetic_only else []
workers, handles, acks, states = [], [], [], []
client = mqtt.Client(mqtt.CallbackAPIVersion.VERSION2)


def start(args, name, cwd=ROOT):
    handle = open(ROOT/f'{name}.log','w')
    handles.append(handle)
    process = subprocess.Popen(args,cwd=cwd,stdout=handle,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
    workers.append(process)
    return process


def wait_http(url, timeout=30):
    deadline = time.time()+timeout
    while time.time()<deadline:
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(url,timeout=2) as response:
                return response.read()
        except OSError:
            time.sleep(.5)
    raise AssertionError('HTTP startup timed out: '+url)


def on_message(client, userdata, message):
    data = json.loads(message.payload)
    if message.topic=='ev/ack':
        acks.append(data)
    elif data.get('kind') != 'history':
        states.append(data)


try:
    for port in (1880, 1883, 8765):
        with socket.socket() as probe:
            probe.settimeout(1)
            if probe.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1',port)) == 0:
                raise RuntimeError(f'Stop the existing project before integration testing: port {port} is busy')
    start(['node','node-red/demo-broker.cjs'],'test-broker')
    service=start([sys.executable,'station_service.py'],'test-service')
    start(['node','node_modules/node-red/red.js','--userDir','.','--settings','settings.js','flows.json'],'test-nodered',ROOT/'node-red')
    wait_http('http://127.0.0.1:8765/health')
    wait_http('http://127.0.0.1:1880/dashboard/station')
    time.sleep(2)
    subprocess.run([sys.executable,sensor_script,'--history',*sensor_flags],cwd=ROOT,check=True,timeout=360)
    deadline=time.time()+20
    while time.time()<deadline:
        health=json.loads(wait_http('http://127.0.0.1:8765/health'))
        if health['active_history_rows']>=432:
            break
        time.sleep(.5)
    assert health['active_history_rows']>=432, 'History did not pass through Node-RED into SQLite'
    if synthetic_only:
        assert health['data_source']=='synthetic-fallback'
    if args.mock_acn:
        assert health['data_source']=='acn-data-caltech'
    print('History committed through MQTT/Node-RED:',health['history_rows'],flush=True)
    with open(ROOT/'training.log','w') as training_log:
        subprocess.run([sys.executable,'ml_train.py'],cwd=ROOT,stdout=training_log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,check=True,timeout=180)
    report=json.loads((ROOT/'artifacts/ml_comparison.json').read_text())
    assert len(report['models'])==5
    # Models are now startup-only; reload our own service after offline training.
    service.terminate()
    service.wait(timeout=10)
    start([sys.executable,'station_service.py'],'test-service-trained')
    wait_http('http://127.0.0.1:8765/health')
    print('Trained five models; selected:',report['selected_model'],flush=True)
    client.on_message=on_message
    client.connect('127.0.0.1',1883)
    client.subscribe('ev/ack',qos=1)
    client.subscribe('ev/telemetry',qos=1)
    client.loop_start()
    start([sys.executable,sensor_script,*sensor_flags],'test-simulator')
    deadline=time.time()+40
    while time.time()<deadline:
        if sum(a['accepted'] for a in acks)>=5 and any(s['applied_sequence']>=0 and s['load_kw']>1 for s in states):
            break
        time.sleep(.5)
    assert sum(a['accepted'] for a in acks)>=5, 'Insufficient accepted actuator plans'
    assert any(s['applied_sequence']>=0 and s['load_kw']>1 for s in states)
    assert max(s['load_kw'] for s in states)<=9.5+1e-6
    time.sleep(1)
    conn=sqlite3.connect(ROOT/'station.db')
    try:
        run=states[-1]['run_id']
        count=conn.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM cycles WHERE run_id=?',(run,)).fetchone()[0]
        ack_count=conn.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM actuator_acks WHERE run_id=?',(run,)).fetchone()[0]
        decision=json.loads(conn.execute('SELECT decision FROM cycles WHERE run_id=? AND decision IS NOT NULL ORDER BY sequence DESC LIMIT 1',(run,)).fetchone()[0])
    finally:
        conn.close()
    assert ack_count>0
    assert decision['model']==report['selected_model']
    assert report['data_source']==health['data_source']
    result={'accepted_commands':sum(a['accepted'] for a in acks),'stored_cycles':count,
            'stored_acknowledgements':ack_count,'history_rows':health['history_rows'],
            'dashboard_http':200,'selected_model':decision['model'], 'data_source':report['data_source'],
            'acn_fixture_test':args.mock_acn,
            'max_observed_load_kw':max(s['load_kw'] for s in states),
            'mean_response_ms':sum(a['response_ms'] for a in acks if a['accepted'])/sum(a['accepted'] for a in acks)}
    times=sorted(a['response_ms'] for a in acks if a['accepted'])
    result['p95_response_ms']=times[min(len(times)-1,int(.95*len(times)))]
    result['mean_inference_ms']=sum(a['inference_ms'] for a in acks if a['accepted'])/len(times)
    output = 'live_check_acn_mock.json' if args.mock_acn else 'live_check.json'
    (ROOT/'artifacts'/output).write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2))
    print(json.dumps(result,indent=2))
finally:
    client.disconnect()
    client.loop_stop()
    for worker in reversed(workers):
        if worker.poll() is None:
            worker.terminate()
            worker.wait(timeout=10)
    for handle in handles:
        handle.close()

"""Integration-test-only ACN fixtures. Never included in the demo submission."""
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
import acn_data
from sender.simulator import main
from test_acn_data import fixture_session

if __name__ == '__main__':
    sessions = [fixture_session(day=d, hour=h, energy=value)
                for d in range(42) for h, value in ((8,60+d%7),(18,10+d%4))]
    with patch.object(acn_data, 'recent_window', return_value=('2020-01-01','2020-02-12')), patch.object(
            acn_data, 'fetch_sessions', return_value=sessions):
        if '--history' in sys.argv:
            # Do not export test fixtures as a real user's prepared fleet.
            with patch.object(acn_data, 'to_vehicle_sessions', return_value=[]):
                main()
        else:
            main()



================================================
FILE: tests/public_mqtt_check.py
================================================
"""Exercise the real public TLS broker with synthetic data and isolated test topics.

Uses existing trained models; writes test cycles to station.db, not training data.
Requires free local ports 1880/8765. Does not start a local broker.
"""
import json
import socket
import sqlite3
import ssl
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import time
import urllib.request
import uuid
from pathlib import Path
import paho.mqtt.client as mqtt

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def main():
    profile = json.loads((ROOT/'mqtt-public.json').read_text())
    prefix = profile['topic_prefix'] + '/check-' + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
    workers, handles, states, acks = [], [], [], []
    subscribed = threading.Event()
    client = mqtt.Client(mqtt.CallbackAPIVersion.VERSION2, client_id='gs_check_'+uuid.uuid4().hex[:12])
    client.tls_set_context(ssl.create_default_context())

    def start(args, name, cwd=ROOT):
        handle = open(ROOT/f'{name}.log', 'w')
        handles.append(handle)
        worker = subprocess.Popen(args, cwd=cwd, stdout=handle, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
                                  creationflags=getattr(subprocess, 'CREATE_NO_WINDOW', 0))
        workers.append(worker)

    def wait_http(url):
        deadline = time.monotonic()+40
        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
            if any(w.poll() is not None for w in workers):
                raise RuntimeError('Test service exited; inspect public-check-*.log')
            try:
                with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=2) as response:
                    return response.status
            except OSError:
                time.sleep(.5)
        raise RuntimeError('Service startup timed out: '+url)

    def connected(c, userdata, flags, reason, properties):
        if reason == 0:
            c.subscribe([(prefix+'/telemetry', 1), (prefix+'/ack', 1)])

    def on_subscribe(c, userdata, mid, reasons, properties):
        if all(not reason.is_failure for reason in reasons):
            subscribed.set()

    def received(c, userdata, message):
        data = json.loads(message.payload)
        (acks if message.topic.endswith('/ack') else states).append(data)

    client.on_connect, client.on_subscribe, client.on_message = connected, on_subscribe, received
    try:
        for port in (1880, 8765):
            with socket.socket() as probe:
                probe.settimeout(1)
                if probe.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', port)) == 0:
                    raise RuntimeError(f'Stop the existing project first; port {port} is busy')
        # This generated flow uses the actual app wiring and the test's isolated topics.
        subprocess.run(['node', '-e',
            "const fs=require('fs');const {buildPublicFlow}=require('./node-red/build-public-flow.cjs');"
            "const p=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('mqtt-public.json'));p.topic_prefix=process.argv[1];"
            "fs.writeFileSync('node-red/flows.public-check.json',JSON.stringify(buildPublicFlow(p,JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('node-red/flows.json')))));",
            prefix], cwd=ROOT, check=True)
        client.connect(profile['host'], profile['port'], 60)
        client.loop_start()
        if not subscribed.wait(25):
            raise RuntimeError('Public broker did not accept observer subscriptions')
        print('Public MQTT TLS connection and subscriptions confirmed.', flush=True)
        start([sys.executable, 'station_service.py'], 'public-check-service')
        wait_http('http://127.0.0.1:8765/health')
        start(['node', 'node_modules/node-red/red.js', '--userDir', '.', '--settings', 'settings.js',
               'flows.public-check.json'], 'public-check-nodered', ROOT/'node-red')
        dashboard = wait_http('http://127.0.0.1:1880/dashboard/station')
        start([sys.executable, 'simulator.py', '--broker-host', profile['host'], '--broker-port',
               str(profile['port']), '--tls', '--topic-prefix', prefix], 'public-check-sensor')
        deadline = time.monotonic()+90
        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
            if any(w.poll() is not None for w in workers):
                raise RuntimeError('A test worker exited; inspect public-check-*.log')
            if sum(a.get('accepted', False) for a in acks) >= 5 and any(s['applied_sequence'] >= 0 for s in states):
                break
            time.sleep(.5)
        accepted = [a for a in acks if a.get('accepted')]
        assert len(accepted) >= 5, 'Fewer than five accepted public-MQTT commands'
        assert any(s['applied_sequence'] >= 0 for s in states), 'Actuator effects not visible in telemetry'
        assert max(s['load_kw'] for s in states) <= 9.5+1e-6
        time.sleep(2)
        run = states[-1]['run_id']
        with sqlite3.connect(ROOT/'station.db') as db:
            stored = db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM cycles WHERE run_id=?', (run,)).fetchone()[0]
            stored_acks = db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM actuator_acks WHERE run_id=?', (run,)).fetchone()[0]
            row = db.execute('SELECT decision FROM cycles WHERE run_id=? AND decision IS NOT NULL ORDER BY sequence DESC LIMIT 1', (run,)).fetchone()
        assert stored >= 5 and stored_acks >= 5 and row
        decision = json.loads(row[0])
        assert decision['model'] != 'untrained_safe_rules'
        result = {'broker': profile['host'], 'port': profile['port'], 'tls_verified': True,
                  'test_scope': 'Local clients using the real internet broker; not two physical laptops',
                  'topic_prefix': prefix, 'accepted_commands': len(accepted), 'stored_cycles': stored,
                  'stored_acknowledgements': stored_acks, 'selected_model': decision['model'],
                  'dashboard_http': dashboard, 'max_observed_load_kw': max(s['load_kw'] for s in states),
                  'mean_response_ms': sum(a['response_ms'] for a in accepted)/len(accepted)}
        (ROOT/'artifacts/public_mqtt_check.json').write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2))
        print(json.dumps(result, indent=2), flush=True)
    finally:
        client.disconnect()
        client.loop_stop()
        for worker in reversed(workers):
            if worker.poll() is None:
                worker.terminate()
                worker.wait(timeout=10)
        for handle in handles:
            handle.close()


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()



================================================
FILE: tests/test_acn_data.py
================================================
import json
import os
import tempfile
import unittest
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import Mock, patch

import acn_data
from sender import station_core
from sender.simulator import history_payload


def fixture_session(day=0, hour=8, energy=10, identifier=None, currents=None):
    start = datetime(2020, 1, 1, hour, tzinfo=timezone(timedelta(hours=-8)))+timedelta(days=day)
    values = currents or [24, 24, 24, 24, 24, 24]
    return {'sessionID': identifier or f'sess{day:04d}{hour:02d}', 'timezone': 'America/Los_Angeles',
            'connectionTime': start.isoformat(), 'disconnectTime': (start+timedelta(hours=2)).isoformat(),
            'kWhDelivered': energy, 'chargingCurrent': {'current': values, 'timestamps':
            [(start+timedelta(hours=2*i/(len(values)-1))).isoformat() for i in range(len(values))]},
            'userInputs': []}


class AcnDataTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.tmp.cleanup)
        self.cache = patch.object(acn_data, 'CACHE_PATH', Path(self.tmp.name)/'cache.json')
        self.local = patch.object(acn_data, 'LOCAL_DIR', Path(self.tmp.name)/'downloads')
        self.local.start()
        self.addCleanup(self.local.stop)
        self.cache.start()
        self.addCleanup(self.cache.stop)
        self.env = patch.dict(os.environ, {}, clear=True)
        self.env.start()
        self.addCleanup(self.env.stop)
        self.network = patch('requests.get', side_effect=AssertionError('Network is forbidden in unit tests'))
        self.network.start()
        self.addCleanup(self.network.stop)
        acn_data.battery_capacity_for.cache_clear()
        acn_data._ev_catalog.cache_clear()

    def test_saved_partial_days_are_live_only(self):
        session = fixture_session()
        acn_data._write_json(acn_data.LOCAL_DIR/'caltech-2020-01-01.json',
                             {'site': 'caltech', 'day': '2020-01-01', 'complete': False,
                              'sessions': [session]})
        self.assertEqual(acn_data.saved_sessions(), [session])
        with self.assertRaises(acn_data.AcnDataError):
            acn_data.saved_sessions(for_history=True)
        with patch.object(acn_data, 'fetch_sessions', side_effect=AssertionError('no network')):
            self.assertEqual(history_payload()['source'], 'synthetic-fallback')
            self.assertEqual(station_core.real_or_synthetic_vehicles()[0].data_source, 'acn-data-caltech')

    def test_saved_history_requires_contiguous_complete_days(self):
        for day in range(18):
            session = fixture_session(day)
            date = acn_data._local(session, 'connectionTime').date().isoformat()
            acn_data._write_json(acn_data.LOCAL_DIR/f'caltech-{date}.json',
                                 {'site': 'caltech', 'day': date, 'complete': True, 'sessions': [session]})
        self.assertEqual(len(acn_data.saved_sessions(for_history=True)), 18)
        (acn_data.LOCAL_DIR/'caltech-2020-01-09.json').unlink()
        with self.assertRaises(acn_data.AcnDataError):
            acn_data.saved_sessions(for_history=True)

    def test_download_keeps_and_resumes_partial_page_progress(self):
        start = datetime(2020, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone(timedelta(hours=-8)))
        first = Mock()
        first.json.return_value = {'_items': [fixture_session()], '_links': {'next': {}}}
        second = Mock()
        second.json.return_value = {'_items': [fixture_session(hour=9)], '_links': {}}
        with patch.dict(os.environ, {'ACN_DATA_TOKEN': 'secret'}), patch.object(
                acn_data, 'recent_window', return_value=(start, start+timedelta(days=1))):
            with patch.object(acn_data, '_get', side_effect=[first, OSError('secret')]):
                self.assertEqual(len(acn_data.download_local(days=1)), 1)
            with patch.object(acn_data, '_get', return_value=second) as get:
                self.assertEqual(len(acn_data.download_local(days=1)), 2)
                self.assertEqual(get.call_args.kwargs['params']['page'], 2)
        record = json.loads((acn_data.LOCAL_DIR/'caltech-2020-01-01.json').read_text())
        self.assertTrue(record['complete'])
        self.assertNotIn('secret', json.dumps(record))

    def test_fetch_sessions_returns_real_sessions_when_token_set(self):
        sessions = [fixture_session(), fixture_session(1, 9)]
        with patch.dict(os.environ, {'ACN_DATA_TOKEN':'private-test-token'}), patch(
                'acnportal.acndata.DataClient.get_sessions', return_value=iter(sessions)) as get:
            result = acn_data.fetch_sessions(start='2020-01-01', end='2020-01-04')
        rows = acn_data.to_demand_samples(result)
        self.assertEqual([(r['day'], r['hour']) for r in rows], [(0,8), (1,9)])
        self.assertTrue(all(r['source']=='acn-data-caltech' for r in rows))
        self.assertTrue(get.call_args.kwargs['timeseries'])
        self.assertIn('Wed, 01 Jan 2020 00:00:00 GMT', get.call_args.kwargs['cond'])
        self.assertNotIn('private-test-token', acn_data.CACHE_PATH.read_text())

    def test_fetch_sessions_raises_when_token_missing(self):
        with self.assertRaises(acn_data.AcnDataNotConfigured):
            acn_data.fetch_sessions(start='2020-01-01', end='2020-01-04')

    def test_simulate_soc_trace_matches_delivered_energy(self):
        trace = acn_data.simulate_soc_trace(fixture_session(), 40, initial_soc=.3)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(trace['final_soc'], .55, delta=.01)
        self.assertEqual(len(trace['soc_series']), 6)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(trace['soc_series'][0]['soc'], .3)
        self.assertEqual(trace['soc_series'][-1]['soc'], trace['final_soc'])
        self.assertTrue(all(a['soc']<=b['soc'] for a,b in zip(trace['soc_series'],trace['soc_series'][1:])))

    def test_taper_detected_above_80_percent(self):
        trace = acn_data.simulate_soc_trace(fixture_session(energy=6, currents=[32]*8+[8,4]), 40, initial_soc=.8)
        self.assertTrue(trace['taper_observed'])
        self.assertAlmostEqual(trace['initial_soc_estimate'], .8)
        self.assertLessEqual(trace['final_soc'], 1)

    def test_requested_energy_and_overnight_session(self):
        session = fixture_session(hour=23)
        session['userInputs'] = [{'kWhRequested': 20}]
        trace = acn_data.simulate_soc_trace(session, 40)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(trace['initial_soc_estimate'], .5)
        self.assertEqual(trace['arrival_hour'], 23)
        self.assertEqual(trace['departure_hour'], 1)
        vehicle = acn_data.to_vehicle_sessions([session], lambda *args: 40)[0]
        self.assertEqual(vehicle.departure, 25)

    def test_real_or_synthetic_history_falls_back_cleanly(self):
        with patch.object(acn_data, 'recent_window', return_value=('2020-01-01','2020-02-12')), patch.object(
                acn_data, 'fetch_sessions', side_effect=OSError('must-not-log-secret')), self.assertLogs(level='WARNING') as captured:
            rows = station_core.real_or_synthetic_history(42)
        self.assertEqual(rows, station_core.historical_sessions(42, seed=42))
        self.assertIn('[FALLBACK] Using synthetic history', '\n'.join(captured.output))
        self.assertNotIn('must-not-log-secret', '\n'.join(captured.output))

    def test_no_token_history_payload_is_explicit_fallback(self):
        payload = history_payload()
        self.assertEqual(payload['source'], 'synthetic-fallback')
        self.assertEqual(len(payload['samples']), 1008)

    def test_insufficient_days_fall_back(self):
        with patch.object(acn_data, 'recent_window', return_value=('2020-01-01','2020-02-12')), patch.object(
                acn_data, 'fetch_sessions', return_value=[fixture_session()]):
            self.assertEqual(station_core.real_or_synthetic_history(42), station_core.historical_sessions(42))

    def test_real_history_is_dense_without_inventing_energy(self):
        sessions = [fixture_session(day=d) for d in range(18)]
        with patch.object(acn_data, 'recent_window', return_value=('2020-01-01','2020-02-12')), patch.object(
                acn_data, 'fetch_sessions', return_value=sessions), patch.object(acn_data, 'to_vehicle_sessions', return_value=[]):
            payload = history_payload()
        self.assertEqual(payload['dataset'], 'acn-caltech-v1')
        self.assertEqual(payload['source'], 'acn-data-caltech')
        self.assertEqual(len(payload['samples']), 18*24)
        self.assertEqual(sum(r['energy_kwh'] for r in payload['samples']), 180)

    def test_synthetic_only_makes_no_fetches(self):
        with patch.object(acn_data, 'fetch_sessions', side_effect=AssertionError('should not fetch')):
            self.assertEqual(history_payload(True)['source'], 'synthetic-fallback')
            self.assertEqual(station_core.real_or_synthetic_vehicles(False), station_core.demo_vehicles())

    def test_cache_ttl_and_minimum_energy_filter(self):
        with patch.dict(os.environ, {'ACN_DATA_TOKEN':'secret'}), patch('acnportal.acndata.DataClient.get_sessions',
                side_effect=lambda *a, **k: iter([fixture_session(energy=1)])) as get:
            with patch('acn_data.time.time', return_value=100):
                self.assertEqual(len(acn_data.fetch_sessions(start='2020-01-01',end='2020-01-03')), 1)
            with patch('acn_data.time.time', return_value=200):
                self.assertEqual(acn_data.fetch_sessions(start='2020-01-01',end='2020-01-03',min_kwh=2), [])
            self.assertEqual(get.call_count, 1)
            with patch('acn_data.time.time', return_value=100+acn_data.CACHE_TTL+1):
                acn_data.fetch_sessions(start='2020-01-01',end='2020-01-03')
            self.assertEqual(get.call_count, 2)

    def test_capacity_lookup_and_unknown_default(self):
        with patch.object(acn_data, '_ev_catalog', return_value=[{'brand':'Example', 'model':'EV', 'year':2020,
                      'battery':{'pack_capacity_kwh_net':58}}]) as catalog:
            self.assertEqual(acn_data.battery_capacity_for('Example','EV',2020), 58)
            self.assertEqual(acn_data.battery_capacity_for('Example','EV',2020), 58)
            self.assertEqual(catalog.call_count, 1)
        with self.assertLogs(level='WARNING'):
            self.assertEqual(acn_data.battery_capacity_for(None,None,None), 40)

    def test_fleet_caps_six_and_disambiguates_ids(self):
        sessions = [fixture_session(identifier=f'samehead{i}') for i in range(8)]
        fleet = acn_data.to_vehicle_sessions(sessions, lambda *args: 40)
        self.assertEqual(len(fleet), 6)
        self.assertEqual(len({v.id for v in fleet}), 6)
        self.assertTrue(all(0<v.max_kw<=7 and v.departure>v.arrival for v in fleet))

    def test_latest_available_window_excludes_partial_day(self):
        response = Mock()
        response.json.return_value = {'_items':[fixture_session(day=41)]}
        with patch.dict(os.environ, {'ACN_DATA_TOKEN':'secret'}), patch.object(acn_data, '_get', return_value=response):
            start, end = acn_data.recent_window(42)
        self.assertEqual(end.hour, 0)
        self.assertEqual((end-start).days, 42)

    def test_end_date_override_is_site_local(self):
        with patch.dict(os.environ, {'ACN_DATA_TOKEN': 'test', 'ACN_DATA_END': '2020-02-12'}):
            start, end = acn_data.recent_window(42)
        self.assertEqual(end.isoformat(), '2020-02-12T00:00:00-08:00')
        self.assertEqual((end-start).days, 42)

    def test_logger_padding_is_trimmed_to_connected_interval(self):
        session = fixture_session()
        series = session['chargingCurrent']
        series['timestamps'].insert(0, (acn_data._datetime(session['connectionTime'])-timedelta(minutes=1)).isoformat())
        series['timestamps'].append((acn_data._datetime(session['disconnectTime'])+timedelta(minutes=1)).isoformat())
        series['current'] = [0]+series['current']+[0]
        trace = acn_data.simulate_soc_trace(session, 40)
        self.assertEqual(len(trace['soc_series']), 6)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(trace['final_soc'], .55)

    def test_invalid_trace_rejected(self):
        session = fixture_session()
        session['chargingCurrent']['timestamps'][2] = session['chargingCurrent']['timestamps'][1]
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            acn_data.simulate_soc_trace(session, 40)

    def test_health_reports_latest_replayed_source(self):
        from station_store import ingest, database
        from station_service import Handler
        db = Path(self.tmp.name)/'health.db'
        handler = Handler.__new__(Handler)
        handler.path = '/health'
        handler.respond = Mock()
        with patch('station_service.database', side_effect=lambda: database(db)):
            for source in ('acn-data-caltech', 'synthetic-fallback', 'acn-data-caltech'):
                ingest({'kind':'history','dataset':source,'source':source,
                        'samples':[{'day':0,'hour':0,'energy_kwh':10}]}, db)
                handler.do_GET()
                self.assertEqual(handler.respond.call_args.args[0]['data_source'], source)

    def test_cache_can_be_unwritable_without_losing_real_results(self):
        with patch.dict(os.environ, {'ACN_DATA_TOKEN':'secret'}), patch(
                'acnportal.acndata.DataClient.get_sessions', return_value=iter([fixture_session()])), patch(
                'pathlib.Path.write_text', side_effect=OSError('denied')):
            result = acn_data.fetch_sessions(start='2020-01-01',end='2020-01-03')
        self.assertEqual(len(result), 1)

    def test_shorter_history_replay_does_not_mix_old_days(self):
        from station_store import ingest, database
        db = Path(self.tmp.name)/'replace.db'
        payload = {'kind':'history','dataset':'acn-caltech-v1','source':'acn-data-caltech',
                   'samples':[{'day':d,'hour':0,'energy_kwh':10} for d in range(3)]}
        ingest(payload, db)
        payload['samples'] = payload['samples'][:1]
        ingest(payload, db)
        with database(db) as conn:
            self.assertEqual(conn.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM demand_samples').fetchone()[0], 1)

FILE: tests/test_fixes.py
================================================
import json
import pickle
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch
import numpy as np
from sender.station_core import Station, schedule
from decision_engine import stabilize, load_bundle, _cache
from ml_train import scores


class FixTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_identical_binary_scores_are_valid_for_different_forecasts(self):
        y=np.array([10.,20.,40.,50.])
        a=scores(y,np.array([11.,21.,39.,49.]))
        b=scores(y,np.array([15.,25.,35.,45.]))
        self.assertEqual(a['f1'],b['f1'])
        self.assertNotEqual(a['rmse_kwh'],b['rmse_kwh'])

    def test_cache_never_reloads_changed_artifact_during_process(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            path=Path(tmp)/'bundle.pkl'
            path.write_bytes(pickle.dumps({'model':None,'baseline':1,'report':{}}))
            original_read=Path.read_bytes
            with patch.object(Path,'read_bytes',autospec=True,side_effect=original_read) as reader:
                first=load_bundle(path)
                path.write_bytes(pickle.dumps({'model':None,'baseline':2,'report':{}}))
                self.assertIs(load_bundle(path),first)
                self.assertEqual(reader.call_count,1)
            _cache.pop(str(path.resolve()))

    def test_edf_feasible_requests_precede_impossible_ones(self):
        state=Station().snapshot()
        plan=schedule(state)
        self.assertEqual(plan['assignments'][0]['ev_id'],'EV1')
        self.assertEqual(plan['assignments'][0]['rate_kw'],7)

    def test_full_congested_run_meets_deadlines_and_margin(self):
        s=Station(); previous=None; switches={}; mapping={}; rapid=0
        for _ in range(185):
            state=s.snapshot()
            plan=stabilize(state,schedule(state),previous)
            self.assertLessEqual(sum(a['rate_kw'] for a in plan['assignments']),8.5+1e-8)
            self.assertTrue(s.accept(plan))
            current={a['charger_id']:a['ev_id'] for a in plan['assignments'] if a['rate_kw']>0}
            for c in s.chargers:
                if mapping.get(c)!=current.get(c):
                    if c in switches and s.hour-switches[c]<.1-1e-8:
                        rapid+=1
                    switches[c]=s.hour
            mapping=current; previous=plan
            s.step(1/30)
            self.assertLessEqual(s.snapshot()['load_kw'],9.5+1e-8)
        self.assertGreater(sum(e.remaining<=.01 for e in s.vehicles),0)
        self.assertEqual(rapid,0)

    def test_actuator_rejects_plan_above_reserved_limit(self):
        s=Station()
        plan=schedule(s.snapshot())
        plan['assignments'][0]['rate_kw']=7
        plan['assignments'][1]['rate_kw']=2
        self.assertFalse(s.accept(plan))

    def test_deadband_holds_rate_without_later_redistribution(self):
        s=Station(); state=s.snapshot()
        state['chargers']=['C1']
        previous={'assignments':[{'ev_id':'EV1','charger_id':'C1','rate_kw':6.9}],'assigned_since':{'EV1':0}}
        plan={'assignments':previous['assignments'],'forecast_factor':1}
        self.assertEqual(stabilize(state,plan,previous)['assignments'][0]['rate_kw'],6.9)

FILE: tests/test_mqtt_config.py
================================================
import ssl
import unittest
import json
import threading
import time
from types import SimpleNamespace
from unittest.mock import Mock, patch

from sender.simulator import configure_connection, apply_public_profile, validate_topic_prefix
from sender.actuator import Actuator


class MqttConfigurationTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_public_profile_drops_private_credentials_and_enables_tls(self):
        args = SimpleNamespace(username='private-account', tls=False)
        apply_public_profile(args)
        self.assertIsNone(args.username)
        self.assertTrue(args.tls)
        self.assertEqual(args.broker_host, 'broker.emqx.io')
        self.assertEqual(args.broker_port, 8883)
        self.assertTrue(args.topic_prefix.startswith('gridsense/team3/'))

    def test_topic_prefix_rejects_wildcards(self):
        for prefix in ('', '/ev', 'ev/', 'ev/#', 'ev/+', 'ev\x00'):
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                validate_topic_prefix(prefix)

    def test_actuator_uses_matching_custom_topics_and_ignores_other_runs(self):
        station = Mock(vehicles=[], chargers=[])
        station.accept.return_value = True
        client = Mock()
        actuator = Actuator(station, 'our-run', threading.Lock(), 'unique/demo')
        actuator.on_connect(client, None, None, 0, None)
        client.subscribe.assert_called_once_with('unique/demo/actuator', qos=1)
        command = {'run_id':'someone-else', 'sequence':1, 'sensor_timestamp':time.time()}
        actuator.on_message(client, None, SimpleNamespace(payload=json.dumps(command)))
        client.publish.assert_not_called()
        station.accept.assert_not_called()
        command['run_id'] = 'our-run'
        actuator.on_message(client, None, SimpleNamespace(payload=json.dumps(command)))
        self.assertEqual(client.publish.call_args.args[0], 'unique/demo/ack')
        self.assertTrue(json.loads(client.publish.call_args.args[1])['accepted'])

    def test_cloud_auth_uses_verified_tls(self):
        client = Mock()
        args = SimpleNamespace(tls=True, ca_cert=None, username='sensor')
        with patch.dict('os.environ', {'MQTT_PASSWORD': 'test-only'}):
            configure_connection(client, args)
        context = client.tls_set_context.call_args.args[0]
        self.assertEqual(context.verify_mode, ssl.CERT_REQUIRED)
        self.assertTrue(context.check_hostname)
        client.username_pw_set.assert_called_once_with('sensor', 'test-only')

    def test_credentials_cannot_be_sent_without_tls(self):
        client = Mock()
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            configure_connection(client, SimpleNamespace(tls=False, ca_cert=None, username='sensor'))
        client.username_pw_set.assert_not_called()

    def test_local_anonymous_demo_still_supported(self):
        client = Mock()
        configure_connection(client, SimpleNamespace(tls=False, ca_cert=None, username=None))
        client.username_pw_set.assert_not_called()
        client.tls_set_context.assert_not_called()

FILE: tests/test_rubric.py

import json
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from sender.station_core import Station
from station_store import ingest, database, read_state
from decision_engine import stabilize, decide
from sender.simulator import history_payload


class RubricTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_independent_dashboard_path_and_topics(self):
        nodes = json.loads((Path(__file__).parents[1]/'node-red/flows.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        nodes = {n['id']:n for n in nodes}
        self.assertEqual(nodes['sensor']['topic'],'ev/telemetry')
        self.assertEqual(set(nodes['sensor']['wires'][0]),{'live-filter','request'})
        self.assertEqual(nodes['actuator']['topic'],'ev/actuator')
        self.assertTrue({'live-dashboard', 'soc-router', 'load-series', 'price-history'} <= set(nodes['live-filter']['wires'][0]))

    def test_db_ingest_idempotent_history(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            db = Path(tmp)/'test.db'
            ingest(history_payload(synthetic_only=True),db)
            ingest(history_payload(synthetic_only=True),db)
            with database(db) as conn:
                self.assertEqual(conn.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM demand_samples').fetchone()[0],1008)

    def test_complete_reference_architecture_wiring(self):
        nodes = {n['id']: n for n in json.loads(
            (Path(__file__).parents[1]/'node-red/flows.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))}
        # Storage must precede analytics; only the decision path drives MQTT output.
        for source, target in [('request', 'analytics'), ('analytics', 'stored'),
                               ('stored', 'db-decision'), ('db-decision', 'route'),
                               ('command', 'actuator')]:
            self.assertEqual(nodes[source]['wires'], [[target]])
        self.assertEqual(nodes['route']['wires'][1], ['command'])
        self.assertTrue(nodes['analytics']['url'].endswith('/store'))
        self.assertTrue(nodes['db-decision']['url'].endswith('/decide'))
        self.assertEqual(nodes['actuator']['type'], 'mqtt out')
        self.assertEqual(nodes['ack']['topic'], 'ev/ack')
        self.assertIn('ack-store', nodes['ack']['wires'][0])
        self.assertTrue(nodes['ack-store']['url'].endswith('/ack'))
        for name in ('sensor', 'actuator', 'ack'):
            self.assertEqual(nodes[name]['broker'], 'broker')

    def test_unstored_state_cannot_drive_decision(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            db = Path(tmp)/'test.db'
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                read_state({'run_id':'absent','sequence':1},db)

    def test_dwell_prevents_unnecessary_reassignment(self):
        s = Station()
        state = s.snapshot()
        state['hour'] = .05
        previous = {'assignments':[{'ev_id':'EV1','charger_id':'C1','rate_kw':4}], 'assigned_since':{'EV1':0}}
        plan = {'assignments':[{'ev_id':'EV2','charger_id':'C1','rate_kw':7}], 'forecast_factor':1}
        state['chargers'] = ['C1']
        result = stabilize(state,plan,previous)
        self.assertEqual(result['assignments'][0]['ev_id'],'EV1')
        state['hour'] = .2
        plan['assignments'] = [{'ev_id':'EV2','charger_id':'C1','rate_kw':7}]
        self.assertEqual(stabilize(state,plan,previous)['assignments'][0]['ev_id'],'EV2')

    def test_safety_overrides_deadband(self):
        s = Station()
        state = s.snapshot()
        state.update(capacity_kw=1.2, hour=.01)
        previous = {'assignments':[{'ev_id':'EV1','charger_id':'C1','rate_kw':4}], 'assigned_since':{'EV1':0}}
        plan = {'assignments':previous['assignments'],'forecast_factor':1}
        result = stabilize(state,plan,previous)
        self.assertLessEqual(sum(a['rate_kw'] for a in result['assignments']),.2)

    def test_untrained_decision_is_explicit(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            db = Path(tmp)/'test.db'
            state = Station().snapshot()
            state.update(run_id='test',timestamp=1)
            key = ingest(state,db)
            decision = decide(key,db,Path(tmp)/'absent.pkl')['decision']
            self.assertEqual(decision['model'],'untrained_safe_rules')
            self.assertEqual(decision['sensor_timestamp'],1)

    def test_models_train_only_from_database_and_report_all_categories(self):
        from ml_train import train
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            db=Path(tmp)/'test.db'
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                train(db,Path(tmp)/'artifacts')
            from acn_data import to_demand_samples
            from test_acn_data import fixture_session
            sessions = [fixture_session(day=d, hour=h, energy=value)
                        for d in range(42) for h, value in ((8, 60+d%7), (18, 10+d%4))]
            sparse = to_demand_samples(sessions)
            bins = {(r['day'],r['hour']):r['energy_kwh'] for r in sparse}
            ingest({'kind':'history', 'dataset':'acn-caltech-v1', 'source':'acn-data-caltech',
                    'samples':[{'day':d,'hour':h,'energy_kwh':bins.get((d,h),0)}
                               for d in range(42) for h in range(24)]}, db)
            report=train(db,Path(tmp)/'artifacts')
            self.assertEqual(report['dataset'], 'acn-caltech-v1')
            self.assertEqual(report['data_source'], 'acn-data-caltech')
            self.assertEqual(len(report['models']),5)
            self.assertIn('traditional_svr', report['models'])
            self.assertIn('ensemble_xgboost', report['models'])
            eligible = [name for name, m in report['models'].items()
                        if m['computational']['inference_p95_ms'] <= 100
                        and m['validation']['rmse_kwh'] <= report['baseline_validation']['rmse_kwh']]
            expected = min(eligible, key=lambda name: report['models'][name]['validation']['rmse_kwh']) if eligible else 'constant_baseline'
            self.assertEqual(report['selected_model'], expected)
            self.assertLess(max(report['training_days']),min(report['validation_days']))
            self.assertLess(max(report['validation_days']),min(report['test_days']))
            for model in report['models'].values():
                self.assertTrue({'accuracy','precision','recall','f1','rmse_kwh','mae_kwh','r2'} <= model['test'].keys())
                self.assertGreater(model['computational']['model_size_bytes'],0)



================================================
FILE: tests/test_station.py
================================================
import json
import sqlite3
import tempfile
import unittest
from contextlib import closing
from pathlib import Path
from sender.station_core import Station, Vehicle, schedule, demand_model
from station_service import process


class StationTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_full_day_constraints_and_departures(self):
        s = Station()
        queued = False
        for _ in range(190):
            state = s.snapshot()
            queued |= any(v["status"] == "queued" and v["arrival"] > 0 for v in state["vehicles"])
            plan = schedule(state)
            self.assertLessEqual(sum(a["rate_kw"] for a in plan["assignments"]), 9)
            self.assertLessEqual(len(plan["assignments"]), 2)
            self.assertTrue(s.accept(plan))
            s.step(1/30)
            self.assertLessEqual(s.snapshot()["load_kw"], 10 + 1e-8)
            for ev in s.vehicles:
                self.assertLessEqual(ev.soc, ev.target + 1e-8)
                if s.hour >= ev.departure:
                    self.assertEqual(ev.power_kw, 0)
                    self.assertEqual(ev.status, "departed")
        self.assertTrue(queued)
        self.assertTrue(any("departed" in a for a in schedule(s.snapshot())["alerts"]))

    def test_atomic_rejection_and_duplicate(self):
        s = Station()
        plan = schedule(s.snapshot())
        bad = json.loads(json.dumps(plan))
        bad["assignments"][0]["rate_kw"] = float("nan")
        self.assertFalse(s.accept(bad))
        self.assertEqual(s.command, {})
        self.assertTrue(s.accept(plan))
        self.assertFalse(s.accept(plan))

    def test_watchdog_and_capacity_drop(self):
        s = Station()
        s.accept(schedule(s.snapshot()))
        s.capacity_kw = 2
        s.step(.05)
        self.assertLessEqual(s.snapshot()["load_kw"], 2 + 1e-8)
        s.step(.1)
        energy = sum(e.delivered_kwh for e in s.vehicles)
        s.step(.1)
        self.assertEqual(energy, sum(e.delivered_kwh for e in s.vehicles))

    def test_no_early_arrivals(self):
        s = Station([Vehicle("future", 1, 2, 10, .5, .6)])
        self.assertEqual(schedule(s.snapshot())["assignments"], [])

    def test_forecast_evaluation(self):
        model = demand_model()
        self.assertEqual(len(model["profile_kwh"]), 24)
        self.assertLess(model["mae_kwh"], model["baseline_mae_kwh"])

    def test_persistence_and_duplicate_delivery(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            db = Path(tmp)/"station.db"
            state = Station().snapshot()
            state["run_id"] = "test"
            process(state, db)
            process(state, db)
            with closing(sqlite3.connect(db)) as conn:
                self.assertEqual(conn.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM cycles").fetchone()[0], 1)
                row = conn.execute("SELECT telemetry,decision FROM cycles").fetchone()
            self.assertEqual(json.loads(row[0])["source"], "synthetic-fallback")
            self.assertIn("assignments", json.loads(row[1]))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()



================================================
FILE: tests/test_upgrades.py
================================================
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch
from sender.station_core import Station, Vehicle, schedule
from tou_scheduler import apply_tou
from sender.tariff import price_at, telemetry_fields


def plan_for(station):
    state = station.snapshot()
    return apply_tou(state, schedule(state, {'profile_kwh': [0.] * 24}))


class UpgradeTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_optimizer_error_falls_back_to_deadline_policy(self):
        s = Station([Vehicle('A', 16, 24, 40, .3, .6)])
        s.hour = 16
        with patch('tou_scheduler.milp', side_effect=RuntimeError('solver unavailable')):
            p = plan_for(s)
        self.assertEqual(p['tou_status'], 'fallback_edf')
        self.assertTrue(s.accept(p))

    def test_tariff_repeats_across_midnight(self):
        self.assertEqual(price_at(23), price_at(24))
        self.assertEqual(price_at(40), price_at(16))
        self.assertGreater(price_at(16), price_at(22))

    def test_flexible_vehicle_waits_for_night(self):
        s = Station([Vehicle('flex', 16, 24, 40, .3, .6)])
        s.hour = 16
        plan = plan_for(s)
        self.assertIn(plan['tou_status'], ('optimal', 'feasible_time_limit'))
        self.assertEqual(plan['assignments'], [])
        s.hour = 22
        self.assertGreater(sum(a['rate_kw'] for a in plan_for(s)['assignments']), 0)

    def test_deadline_overrides_expensive_price(self):
        s = Station([Vehicle('urgent', 16, 17, 10, .1, .8)])
        s.hour = 16
        p = plan_for(s)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(p['assignments'][0]['rate_kw'], 7, places=5)
        self.assertTrue(s.accept(p))

    def test_shared_socket_requires_early_charging(self):
        s = Station([Vehicle('A', 21, 23, 10, .1, .6),
                     Vehicle('B', 21, 23, 10, .1, .6)], chargers=1)
        s.hour = 21
        p = plan_for(s)
        self.assertIn(p['tou_status'], ('optimal', 'feasible_time_limit'))
        # Only 7 kWh fits in the cheap hour; 3 kWh must be delivered earlier.
        self.assertLessEqual(len(p['assignments']), 1)
        self.assertGreater(sum(a['rate_kw'] for a in p['assignments']), 0)

    def test_impossible_target_is_explicit(self):
        s = Station([Vehicle('impossible', 16, 17, 40, .1, .8)])
        s.hour = 16
        p = plan_for(s)
        self.assertEqual(p['tou_status'], 'fallback_edf')
        self.assertTrue(any('feasibility' in a for a in p['alerts']))
        self.assertTrue(s.accept(p))

    def test_future_arrival_not_assigned_now(self):
        s = Station([Vehicle('future', 22, 24, 40, .3, .6)])
        s.hour = 16
        self.assertEqual(plan_for(s)['assignments'], [])

    def test_price_fields_do_not_change_station_payload(self):
        s = Station().snapshot()
        extra = telemetry_fields(s)
        self.assertNotIn('price_per_kwh', s)
        self.assertEqual(extra['price_currency'], 'INR')
        self.assertEqual(extra['charging_load_kw'], 0)

    def test_paired_demo_meets_deadlines_and_saves_cost(self):
        from evaluate_tou import evaluate
        r = evaluate()['results']
        self.assertTrue(all(v['deadlines_met'] for v in r.values()))
        self.assertLess(r['tou']['charging_cost_inr'], r['edf']['charging_cost_inr'])
        self.assertLess(r['tou']['peak_grid_kw'], r['unmanaged']['peak_grid_kw'])
        self.assertLessEqual(r['tou']['peak_grid_kw'], 9.5 + 1e-6)
        self.assertEqual(r['tou']['fallback_cycles'], 0)
